公司簡介 
東陽成立於 1967 年，主營項目為汽、機車
零組件 ， 為台灣塑膠件供應商 ， 也是全球第
一大汽車 AM 塑膠件製造商，塑膠件全球
市佔率 70%、 板金部份市佔率38%。2Q26 
AM 營收佔比 75.68%，OEM 為 24.32%。 
AM 營收分布為北美 71%、中南美 11%、
歐洲 11%、中東 3%。OEM 市場為中國
64%、台灣 36%。

主要客戶：一汽 64%、特斯拉 11%、長安
汽車 10%、廣汽 4% 
主要競爭對手：

張予柔 yurou.chang@sinopac.com 
本次報告：電話訪談

東陽 (1319 TT) 
AM 產業長期正向

永豐觀點 
預估 2027 年 AM 營收增長 5~8%、OEM 年增 1~3%。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進建議 ：預估 2026、2027 年營收為 242.9、261 億元(-3.2%、+7.5%YoY)、
毛利率 32.7%、34%，營業利益為 40.5、49 億元(-12%、+21%YoY)，稅後淨利
為 36.1、40.2 億元(-4.9%、+11%YoY)，稅後 EPS 6.12、6.79 元。看好東陽最差
情況已過，即將迎來旺季，出貨將逐步重返正常增長軌道。目前評價交易在
13X2026 年 EPS(F)、11.9X2027 年 EPS(F)，為近五年本益比區間(10~18X)下緣，
本次以 2027 年 EPS 進行評價，給予 15X 本益比，目標價 102 元(15X2027 年(F) 
EPS 6.79 元)，維持買進建議。

ESG 評析 
東陽企業永續評鑑整體為 B+等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 B+等與 B+等。

公司簡介：東陽成立於 1967 年，主營項目為汽、機車零組件，為台灣塑膠件供應
商，也是全球第一大汽車 AM 塑膠件製造商，塑膠件全球市佔率 70%、板金部份
市佔率 38%。2Q26 AM 營收佔比 75.68%，OEM 為 24.32%。AM 營收分布為北
美 71%、中南美 11%、歐洲 11%、中東 3%。OEM 市場為中國 64%、台灣 36%。
1995 年起，公司陸續在中國重慶、南京、福州、長春、哈爾濱、敦陽、長春、天
津、武漢、長沙、廣州等地設廠，中國 OEM(非合併)主要客戶包含一汽 64%、特
斯拉 11%、長安汽車 10%、廣汽 4%。 
關稅底定，北美 AM 客戶回補庫存，3Q26 AM 營收年增 15.7%，重回成長：美
國 5 月下旬將台灣輸美汽車零件的 232 條款關稅調降至 15% 後，客戶結束觀
望，回補先前壓低的庫存，拉貨逐月增加，目前東陽工廠稼動率逾 90%，採兩班
制 24 小時生產。3Q26 AM 營收 45 億元(+5% QoQ、+15.7% YoY)，9 月 AM 營
收 14.96 億元(+0.85% MoM、+7.6% YoY)。 
AM 產業長期正面：北美維修量降幅收斂、副廠件占比創高；北美旺季即將到
來，客戶補庫存可期：北美碰撞維修量已從 2025 年的大幅衰退回到接近持平，
副廠件占比創新高，產業面已落底，結構變化對東陽長期有利： 
1. 汽車維修產業面：維修量衰退已收斂，且AM 件占比創新高：依美國理賠估價
平台 CCC Intelligent Solutions 統計(下簡稱 CCC)，2026 年 1~9 月全美理
賠件數降幅由去年同期的 8.3%縮小至 3.5%，可修復估價件數由 10.4%縮小
至 5.4%。北美大型汽車維修連鎖企業 Boyd 估計 2Q26 可修復理賠量持平至
年減 2%，與 CCC 方向一致。另，依 CCC 統計，2025 年副廠件占全美碰撞
換件金額 22.7%，為歷史新高；在維修量下滑的環境下，副廠件金額仍成長  
1.7%，原廠件則減少 1.8%。綜合上述，產業面已落底，有望回溫，而結構方
面，副廠件使用率持續提升，東陽為受惠者。 
2. 東陽客戶面：主要客戶 LKQ 於北美 AM 碰撞件優於市場：LKQ 2Q26 北美
零件及服務有機營收成長 0.5%，為九季來首度轉正，其中 AM 碰撞件營收
成長約 2%；同時，LKQ 公布其替代零件使用率(APU，即維修時選用副廠件、
而非原廠件的比例)連續兩季創下歷史新高，已突破 40%，背後原因是美國保
險公司近期理賠成本上升壓力較大，加上二手車價格走高，促使保險公司更傾

營運現況與分析 
圖一：東陽主要生產汽車塑膠及鈑金件

資料來源：東陽，Oct. 2026

### Page 3

3

向要求維修廠使用價格較低的副廠零件以控制成本 ， 顯示副廠零件滲透率提升
是長期趨勢，而非短期景氣波動。 
3. 研究部研判：AM 最差情況已過。維修量降幅收斂、副廠件占比提升，加上關
稅底定後的補庫存，再加上東陽每年投入平均約 18 億元開發新模具(今年為
25 億元)、持續擴大北美與歐洲保險公司認證，支撐 AM 營收回到成長軌道、
長期向好。惟 4Q25 受暴風雪帶動、基期較高，若今年冬天偏暖，則 4Q26
年增幅度可能有限；1Q27 則因 1Q26 低基期，年增表現較有利。 
短期負面因子：貨櫃仍偏緊，緩解速度取決於旺季強度：目前待出貨訂單累積 200
多櫃訂單，等待客戶安排貨櫃艙位，緩解速度需視旺季強度(採 FOB 報價，過往客
戶在需求好的時候願意加價取得貨櫃)。研究處認為目前客戶庫存約 1 個月，且旺
季(11 月~隔年 3 月)到來，預期有望逐步緩解。 
4Q26 毛利率有機會優於 3Q26：旺季稼動率有機會達 90%~95%(目前東陽工廠
稼動率逾 90%)，可攤提模具折舊等固定成本(AM 模具折舊約占 AM 成本 17%)。
旺季時北美銷售比重由淡季約 65%~66%回升至約 70%，有助產品組合。而市場
擔憂原物料上升的部分，截至九月來看，PP 較戰前上漲 5%，ABS 為 24%，但因
塑膠原料約 90%使用 PP、ABS 僅約 10%， 且東陽議價能力強 ， 成本上漲可轉嫁，
今年以來東陽產品售價已調整約 1.1%~1.5%，占比近 30%的自有產品轉嫁較快，
其餘產品約需一季~半年轉嫁，綜合以上，原物料上漲的部分對整體毛利率影響不
大。主要影響毛利率的因素在於產品組合(北美營收佔比、塑膠件比重)，以及稼動
率，故預期 4Q26、1Q27AM 毛利率將有望回升至 40%以上。 
預估 2027 年東陽 AM 營收重回正常成長軌道，年增 5~8%；OEM 年增 1~3%：
東陽是全球第一大汽車 AM 塑膠件製造商，塑膠件全球市佔率 70%、板金部份市
佔率 38%，其品項覆蓋範圍最廣，且模具持續增加，故在市況不明朗時，東陽仍
佔據產業領先地位及強議價能力，東陽2026 年預估將有 500~600 項 AM 新品通
過認證，新產品毛利率較高，搭配 Q4~Q1 迎來傳統旺季，預估 AM 營收有望持
續回溫。AM 市場的競爭重點便是模具數及規模經濟，參考東陽於 2024-2025 年
擴大模具開發套數及擴充產能，將有助於未來幾年 AM 營收的增長。而 OEM 方
面，台灣車市有望逐步復甦，且東陽客戶新案陸續放量，有望支撐2027 年營運表
現。中國 OEM 方面，今明年重點在於電動車轉型持續發酵，客戶展望正面，雖然
前幾年陸續關廠導致營收規模持續縮減， 但近期已調整告一段落， 預期整體產能利
用率能夠提升，推升毛利率表現。綜合以上，研究部預估2027 年東陽 AM 營收重
回正常成長軌道，年增 5~8%；OEM 年增 1~3%。 
維持買進建議，長線投資價值顯現：預估 2026、2027 年營收為 242.9、261 億元
(-3.2%、+7.5%YoY)、毛利率 32.7%、34%，營業利益為 40.5、49 億元(-12%、
+21%YoY)，稅後淨利為 36.1、40.2 億元(-4.9%、+11%YoY)，稅後 EPS 6.12、

### Page 1

みずほ経済 EXPRESS

みずほ GDP ナウ（2026 年 10 月中旬時点） 
～7～9 月期 GDP は前期比＋0.03％（年率＋0.10％）と推計～

調査部  シニア日本経済エコノミスト 越山祐資 
同             エコノミスト  阿部大樹 
080-1069-4835 yusuke.koshiyama@mizuho-rt.co.jp 
■ 10 月中旬時点の「みずほ GDP ナウ」は 7～9 月期 GDP を前期比＋0.10％と予測 
みずほ総合研究所では景気動向をタイムリーに把握するべく、浦沢（2023）等を参考にGDPナウキ
ャスティングに取り組んできた。これを「みずほGDPナウ」と呼称することとし、太田他（2024）で
はデータドリブン予測である「みずほGDPナウ」が民間予測平均並みの予測精度を確保できることを
示した。その上で、酒井他（2024）をはじめとして、月次経済指標を用いたGDPナウキャスティング
の結果を紹介してきたところである。 
本稿では10月中旬時点までに得られる月次経済指標を用い、 「みずほGDPナウ」 による7～9月期GDP
の予測結果を紹介する。予測に用いたデータは、8月分の鉱工業生産、 消費活動指数、 所定外労働時間、
消費財出荷指数、第3次産業活動指数、9月分の中小企業景況調査（売上げ見通しDI）である1。図表1
には、予測に使用した各月次経済指標の推移を示している。 
図表 1 各月次経済指標の推移

モデルによる10月中旬時点における7 ～9月期実質GDP成長率の予測値は、図表2のとおり前期比＋
0.03％ （年率＋0.10％）となった。 他機関による予測では、東京財団政策研究所による10月15日時点の
ナウキャスティングが同＋0.29％（年率＋1.18％）、日本経済研究センターが公表した10月のESPフォ
ーキャスト調査（回答期間：2026年9月30日～10月7日、回答者37名）における民間予測値平均が前期
比年率＋0.08％となっている。「みずほGDPナウ」の予測値は、民間コンセンサスよりやや高いもの
の、概ね近い伸びを予測していると言える。 
なお、多くの民間エコノミストが保守的な見方をしている理由の一つには、GDP統計作成上のテク
ニカルな要因がある。4～6月期には、 中東情勢悪化を背景に原油 ・ ナフサ輸入が大幅に減少したほか、
石油化学製品の供給途絶懸念から幅広い企業で在庫積み増しの動きが生じた。やや直感に反するが、
いずれもGDPに対しては押し上げ要因となる。 そのため、4～6月期GDPは前期比＋0.36％ （年率＋1.45％）
とプラス成長になったものの、 成長率が示すほど景気の実態は強くなかったとみるのが自然であろう。
反対に、7～9月期については、4～6月期の反動で輸入増・在庫減の動きが顕在化し、GDPには一定の
下方圧力がかかる可能性がある。みずほ総合研究所が9月に公表したレポート2でも、こうしたテクニ
カル要因を考慮の上、7～9月期GDPは横ばい圏の伸びにとどまると予想している。 
■ 「みずほ GDP ナウ」による予測値下方修正の主因は生産・消費関連指標の悪化 
「みずほGDPナウ」 による7～9月期のGDP（1次速報値） の予測にあたっては10月中旬までに公表さ
れた月次経済指標を使って予測値を4回更新した。図表3では、予測値の更新過程と各月次経済指標の
寄与度を示している3。予測値が下方修正された主因は、8月の鉱工業生産、 消費財出荷指数の低下だ。
特に鉱工業生産指数については、7月末に発生した熊本地震による影響が下押し要因になったとみら
れる。 大手自動車メーカーで工場の操業が停止した影響で自動車が前月比▲6.8％と大幅に減産したほ
か、 電子部品 ・ デバイスも同▲1.9％の減産となった。7月調査時点の生産予測指数は前月比＋3.1％（ 補
図表 2 各機関の実質GDP成長率予測値 図表 3 予測値の改定過程と各経済指標の寄与度

### Page 3

3

正値ベース）と増産が見込まれていたが、大幅に下振れた格好だ。一方、世界的に旺盛なAI・データ
センター関連需要を背景に、半導体製造装置を含む生産用機械などが増産したものの、熊本地震の影
響を打ち返せなかった。消費財出荷指数の低下については、4～5月の耐久財特需が一巡したことで反
動減の動きが生じた。非耐久財の出荷は前月から増加したものの、7月の落ち込みも踏まえると、 基調
としては力強さを欠いていると言えよう。長雨や低温、といった天候不順により生鮮野菜価格が上昇
しているほか、食肉も値上がりが大きく、こうした手に取る機会が多い品目の価格上昇が家計の節約
志向を強め、個人消費の下押し圧力となっているとみられる。9月についても、食品のほか、原油・石
油化学製品等のコスト高の影響で幅広い品目の価格上昇が加速する見込みであり、個人消費は冴えな
い動きが続く可能性がある。 
以上のように、7～9月期成長率は低い伸びに抑えられる公算が大きい。前述のとおり原油・ナフサ
の輸入増、 企業の在庫調整といった動きはGDP統計上、成長率に対してマイナスに寄与する。加えて、
熊本地震による生産減や天候要因による消費支出抑制の動きが下押し要因になる見込みだ。 
次回の 「みずほGDPナウ」については、11月16日に公表される7～9月期GDP1次速報を挟んで、10～
12月期GDPのリアルタイム予測に取り組む。10月分の鉱工業生産や消費活動指数の結果等を踏まえて
推計し、12月中旬頃のレポート発刊を予定している。原油高等を受けた物価上昇圧力の強まりや堅調
なAI・データセンター関連需要など、プラス・マイナス双方の影響が交錯する中、ナウキャストによ
る景気動向の把握はより重要なものとなるだろう。

1 ステップワイズ法により使用データを採択した。詳細は太田ほか（2024）を参照されたい。今後のモデルの予測精度のパフォ
ーマンス評価等を踏まえ、採択するデータについては見直しを行う可能性がある。 
2 詳細については、服部直樹「年率＋1.4％と 1 次速報から小幅に上方修正（4～6 月期2 次QE）」（2026 年9 月8 日）を参照され
たい。 
3 太田他（2024）で示した枠組みと同様、図表 2 の折れ線が各時点における実質GDP 成長率の予測値であり、月次の経済指標が
新たに公表されたり改定されたりすることで予測値が更新される。棒グラフは、予測値の改定幅、すなわち前回予測との差を各
月次経済指標で寄与度分解したもので、寄与度を合計するとモデル予測値の改定幅と一致する。なお、 「みずほGDP ナウ」は各
月次経済指標についても公表前に予測値を計算しており、各指標の寄与度はモデル予測値と実績値の乖離から計算されることか
ら、寄与度の符号は経済指標の変化の方向とは必ずしも一致しない。

◼ TOPIX 啟動大規模改革：日本交易所集團 10 月 7 日公布 TOPIX 調整結果，符合新制納入標
準的成分股將由 9 月底的 1,636 檔降至 986 檔，另有 35 檔股票新納入。現有成分股中，共
有 683 檔未符合流動性及浮動市值等標準，這些股票將自 2026 年 10 月底起逐步降低權
重，並於 2027 年 10 月重新評估，屆時仍未達標者將於 2028 年 7 月底降至零。此次改革有
助於改善 TOPIX 成分股的流動性及投資價值，並促使企業更加重視市場評價。

◼ 巴西總統選舉波索納洛首輪領先魯拉：巴西最高選舉法院 10 月 5 日公布首輪投票結果，參
議員弗拉維奧・波索納洛取得 47.03%的有效票，現任總統魯拉獲得 45.16%，兩人均未取得
過半票數，將於 10 月 25 日進行第二輪決選。若最終由波索納洛當選將相較有利於巴西股市
表現。
◼ IEA 支持加速釋出既有儲油：國際能源署（IEA）10 月 7 日表示，會員國支持加快釋出 3 月
已承諾提供的儲油，並將優先增加柴油供應。截至目前，會員國已釋出約 3.25 億桶儲油；若
剩餘承諾全數執行，可再向市場提供約 1 億桶供應，有助緩解運輸業與農業面臨的燃料成本
壓力。隨各國持續釋出戰略儲備石油，將有利於在短期內壓抑全球油價進一步上行。
市場展望
◼ 標普 500 獲利預估連續上修，資訊科技為主要推升來源：標普 500 企業獲利預估連續兩季上
修，其中資訊科技產業為主要改善來源，反映 AI、雲端運算及資料中心需求仍然強勁。雖然
美國資料中心仍面臨電力與基礎設施瓶頸，但建置規模持續擴大，AI 投資也逐步延伸至記憶

體、網路設備、資安及企業應用等領域。因此，預期科技類股的獲利動能仍具支撐，具 AI 供
應鏈優勢的台股與日股亦可望延續相關成長動能。
◼ 中天期債券可望兼顧息收與利率風險：美國經濟仍具韌性，加上政府與企業發債供給維持高
檔，可能使市場利率維持在較高水準。雖然投資級債信用利差位於相對低檔，但企業獲利、
現金流及償債能力仍具支撐，較高的美國公債基準利率也使其利息收益維持一定水準。短天
期債券面臨利率下降後的再投資風險，長天期債券則較易受到公債供給及利率波動影響，因
此中天期美國公債及投資級債可望在息收與利率風險之間維持較為平衡的表現。

⚫ 澳洲央行決議如預期升息一碼至 4.60%為今年第四度升息，基準利率創 2011 年
以來新高，澳洲央行在政策聲明中表示通膨壓力尚未緩解，不排除在11、12 月

⚫ 美國第二季美 GDP 終值從 1.5%上修至 2.2%， 其中實質個人消費支出創下2025
年 3 月以來最大單月增幅，顯示即使高利率持續，美國家庭消費依然具韌性；
另一方面，美國 8 月核心 PCE 物價指數年增月增 0.2%、3.0%，雙雙低於市場預
期的 0.3%、3.3%，美國 9 月非農就業人數新增 2.9 萬人低於預期的 9 萬人，失
業率由 4.1%上升至 4.2%，8 月非農就業人數亦同步下修至 13.3 萬人，通膨下滑
和勞動力市場略有降溫，降低市場對聯準會 10 月升息預期，Fedwatch 顯示 10

月升息機率下降至 20%左右。
⚫ 受美伊衝突引發的能源價格衝擊影響 ，9 月歐元地區消費者物價調和指數(HICP)
整體上升速度遠超預期，其中，法國年增 3.4%、德國年增 3.3%，歐元區年增
3.8%，均高於歐洲央行的 2%目標，反映能源價格的衝擊持續影響物價，加深歐
洲央行再次升息的壓力。
⚫ 日本上週公佈貨幣政策會議紀要及央行意見摘要顯示多名委員認為當前政策利
率依然處於中性區間之下 ，需警惕通膨上行風險 ， 且未來應該以更快速度升息抑
制通膨，然而意見摘要提及日本經濟財政大臣城內實表示希望央行充分審視過
去升息對景氣之影響 ，市場解讀為政府牽制日本央行未來升息步伐 ， 而財務大臣
片山皋月則重申尊重日本央行的自主性。

 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Oct/5/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週台幣匯價高點在 31.760，低點在 31.946，以 31.830 作收，較前一週收盤價
貶值 5 分。 
上週台幣震盪走貶，週初，國際油價持穩於每桶 100 美元上方，市場預期聯準
會後續仍有升息的空間，提振美元指數表現，加以外資賣超台股並匯出，台幣走貶
至 31.900 上方；不過適逢月底也是季底，匯價逢高吸引出口商進場賣美金，緩解台
幣貶值的速度；臨近週末，美國 8 月核心 PCE 數據 3%低於預期 3.3%，但市場等待
9 月非農就業數據，交投偏向謹慎觀望，最終在央行調節之下收在 31.830。

＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週台幣區間震盪。就國際市場而言，美國 9 月非農就業人數新增 2.9 萬
人，低於市場預期 9 萬人，且失業率由 4.1%上升至 4.2%，顯示就業市場動能略有
減弱，市場對於 10 月升息的預期下降，但仍預期 12 月會再度升息。美元指數雖然
脫離高點，但仍在 102 附近高位震盪，預計將給台幣帶來壓力；就台灣市場來看，
台股再創收盤新高且外資轉為買超台股，且隨著 AI 需求持續帶動台灣經濟出口表
現強勁，預計出口商後續拋匯需求將持續提供台幣支撐。綜合以上，預估本週台幣
區間震盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：31.600-32.000

上週人民幣震盪偏升。隨著美中兩國完成新一輪談判，雙方同意延長休兵，從
原定的 11 月延長到明年 1 月 ， 降低短期貿易摩擦升溫的風險 ， 支撐人民幣的走勢由
6.7170 走升至 6.7030；不過由於美伊戰事持續 ，國際油價持穩於每桶 100 美元上方 ，
加以美元指數及美債收益率雙雙走高 ， 令人民幣由升轉貶至6.7200 上方 ； 臨近週末，
美國公布 9 月非農數據降溫，聯準會10 月升息預期下滑，美元指數回落，境外人民
幣走升至 6.7000 週內高點，終場境外人民幣收在 6.7061，週線升值 166 點，境內人
民幣於十一長假前夕收盤於在 6.7053，週線升值 72 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
    預估本週人民幣區間震盪 。 就國際市場來看 ， 美國 9 月非農就業人數新增 2.9 萬
人，遠低於市場預期 9 萬人，且失業率由 4.1%上升至 4.2%，顯示就業市場動能略
為減弱。數據公布後，市場對於聯準會10 月升息的預期下降，美元指數小幅回落，
有助於人民幣的表現；就中國國內市場來看，人行中間價格再度升破 6.7400，有助
於人民幣的走勢 ， 不過10/1~10/7 是中國十一長假 ， 預計人民幣走勢主要將受國際美
元影響；最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH 日線圖中，各天期均線仍向下發散，空頭格
局不變，不過匯價反彈至 20 日均線附近，顯示下行動能暫緩。綜合以上，預估本週
人民幣區間震盪。

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週 USD/JPY 震盪上行。週初，美元指數持穩在 101 以上高位，USD/JPY 大致
在 156.50 至 157.85 間波動；週間，適逢日本上半年會計年度結算，出口商拋匯使資
金匯回力道強勁 ， 帶動USD/JPY 下行最低來到 156.35，但逢低有買盤進場，USD/JPY
回到 157 整數關卡之上，後隨美債殖利率創高、美元指數突破 102 關卡，USD/JPY
上行最高來至 158.45；臨近週末，美國公布 9 月非農就業人數低於預期，USD/JPY
一度回落至 157 下方，終場收在 157.83，週線上漲 57 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY 區間震盪 ， 主要原因如下 ： 一 、日本 9 月製造業 PMI 由 54.9
回落至 54.1，增長速度放慢至六個月以來的低位，主因為產出和新訂單增長放緩，
經濟復甦力道可能減緩，不利日圓表現。二、美國 8 月核心 PCE 物價指數年增 3.0%，
美國 9 月非農數據 2.9 萬人均低於預期，通膨和勞動力市場略有降溫使市場對聯準
會升息預期下滑，加之部分聯準會官員表示無需急於進一步升息，Fedwatch 顯示 10
月升息機率降至兩成，然而美國第二季 GDP 上修至 2.2%經濟基本面穩健仍將支撐
美元。三、就技術面而言，日線圖中，USD/JPY 匯價處於區間波動，且各天期均線
趨平，上方 158.50 有年線、季線壓力，下方 156.25 則有月線支撐，預期短線以區間
震盪可能性較高。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY 本週呈現區間震盪。

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 下跌。週初，美元指數持穩在 101 以上高位，EUR/USD 震盪下
行至 1.1310，而在紐約聯儲主席威廉姆斯發表不急於再次升息的偏鴿言論後，
EUR/USD 反彈至 1.1340；週間，美國 8 月 PCE 數據低於預期，EUR/USD 一度反彈
至 1.1380，然而在全球債券拋售潮下，資金回流美元帶動美元指數突破 102 關卡，
EUR/USD 跌破 1.1300 關卡最低來至 1.1214；臨近週末，美國 9 月非農就業人數低
於預期，EUR/USD 反彈至 1.1285 後回落，終場收在 1.1253，週線下跌 138 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
預估本週 EUR/USD 震盪偏弱，主要原因如下：一、上週公佈歐元地區消費者
物價調和指數(HICP)，法國年增 3.4%、德國年增 3.3%、歐元區年增 3.8%，顯示能
源價格衝擊依然使通膨壓力居高不下， 市場預期歐洲央行可能在 12 月再度升息 ， 將
帶給歐元部分支撐。二、美國 8 月核心 PCE 物價指數年增 3.0%、月增 0.2%低於預
期，而 9 月非農就業人數 2.9 萬人低於預期的 9 萬人 ， 失業率則由 4.1%上升至 4.2%，
就業降溫及通膨回落降低市場對聯準會十月升息的預期，然而第二季美國 GDP 終
值從 1.5%上修至 2.2%，經濟基本面持穩仍有利美元維持強勢。三、技術面而言，在
日線圖中，EUR/USD 匯價在各天期均線下方並跌破 1.1300 整數關卡，月線向下穿
過季線形成死亡交叉，顯示短線空方動能較強。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD 本週呈
現震盪偏弱的可能性較高。

聯發科 (2454 TT) 
ASIC 業務引領營運成長

永豐觀點 
ASIC 業務營收規模優於預期，2028 年還有第二個 ASIC 客戶步入量產(屬於 AI 機
櫃設計等級，公司能承接的工作範圍更多)，帶動營運顯著成長。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進：ASIC 業務貢獻優於預期，研究部上修獲利，預估聯發科 2026 年稅後
EPS 72.8 元(原預估 70.5 元)，2027 年稅後 EPS 142.78 元(原預估 132.52 元)，
2028 年稅後 EPS 321.22 元(原預估 312.93 元)。聯發科 ASIC 業務進展優於預期，
可望帶動後續營運強勁成長，研究部對聯發科維持買進，目標價由5570 元調升至
5800 元(由 2027~2028 年 EPS 均值與 25 倍評價)。

ESG 評析 
聯發科企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向
平均得分分別為 A 等與 A 等。

聯發科為 台系 IC 設計龍頭廠商 ：聯發科為全球第五大 IC 設計業者 (僅次於
Qualcomm、Broadcom、nVidia)，著墨智慧型手機、智慧裝置與電源管理晶片的
發展 。 在智慧型手機晶片方面的主要競爭對手為Qualcomm、 展訊及華為海思等 ，
在智慧家庭、AIoT 等應用領域則以 Broadcom、Marvell、聯詠及瑞昱等廠商為
主。客戶主要透過代理商供應產品，終端客戶以全球手機品牌(OPPO、VIVO、小
米、Samsung、榮耀等)以及智慧家電品牌為主。 
3Q26 財報優於預期 ：聯發科 3Q26 營收 1680.58 億元(+10%QoQ，+18%YoY)，
毛利率 46.3%， 營業利益率16.1%， 稅後淨利278.82 億元(+15%QoQ，+11%YoY)，
稅後 EPS 17.37 元，財報優於預期，主要是營收超標、營業費用控管得宜所致。 
聯發科 3Q26 財報各個細項表現如下：(1) 營收季增 10%，超逾目標值 1522 億元
~1598 億元(QoQ+0%~+5%)，三大產品線表現均優於預期，得益於專案挹注程
度與供應鏈產能爭取進度較佳，三大產品線以Smart Edge Platform 挹注最顯著。
聯發科 3Q26 三 大 產 品 線 營 運 動 能 如 下 ：Smart edge platform 季增
20%(+68%YoY， 營收佔比58%)、 電源晶片(+1%QoQ，+18%YoY， 營收佔比5%)、
手機營收滑落 1%QoQ(-17%YoY，營收佔比 37%)，其中 Smart edge platform
季增幅度超逾公司原預期的，QoQ+5%~+9%，公司前次法說即已表示將有多項
聯網與車載項目於 3Q26 進入量產 ， 現在看來5G modem/AI tablet(天璣 9500 導
入三星平板電腦 Tab S120/AI PC / 車載晶片貢獻更優，並且 TV SoC 內含 DRAM
漲價亦有貢獻。 電源晶片亦優於公司原預期的持平， 主要是來自運算與資料中心市
占率提升。手機僅季減1%，落於公司原預期的 QoQ-5%~+0%的上緣，應受惠於
旗艦晶片天璣 9600 出台所致。(2) 毛利率 46.3%，與前季相仿，落於目標值區間
44.5%~47.5%區間。(3) 營業費用 507 億元(+7%QoQ)，營業費用成長性<營收
成長性，反映營業費用控管得宜。(4) 聯發科 3Q26 存貨 1535 億元(+56%QoQ)，
庫存天數由 2Q26 的 102 天增至 127 天，主要是因應 ASIC 業務拓展。

4Q26 營收展望優於預期：聯發科預估 4Q26 營收 2101 億元~2218 億元(以美元
對新台幣匯率 31.7 元計算)，QoQ+25%~+32%，YoY+40% ~+48%，毛利率目
標值區間下滑 1%至 43.5%~46.5%，營業費用率 24%~28%，反映營業利益率
16%~23%，優於過往三年多落於 14%~19%水準。聯發科 4Q26 展望細項如下：
(1) 4Q26 營運由 ASIC 業務驅動，(2) 4Q26 毛利率目標值區間下滑 1%，主要是受
ASIC 業務毛利率<公司均值，以及晶片價格因製造成本上升而出現轉嫁事宜的綜
合因素影響。(3) ASIC 業務的營業利益率>公司均值，故帶動公司 4Q26 營業利益
率回升。(4) 公司預期 2026 年營收成長性，超逾原預期的 YoY+5%~+9%。研究
部預估聯發科 4Q26 營收 2180 億元(＋30%QoQ，+45%YoY)，毛利率 45%，稅
後獲利 405.2 億元(+45%QoQ，+77%YoY)，稅後 EPS 25.25 元。 
ASIC 業務貢獻值優於預期：聯發科本次法說揭示 ASIC 業務貢獻值優於預期：(1) 
公司預期 2026 年 ASIC 業務 25 億美元(優於前次法說僅預期 >20 億美元)，
Zebrafish 已在 09/2026 開始小量出貨。(2) 2027 年 ASIC 業務 160 億美元(市佔
率 20%)，落於公司原預期的市占率 15%~20%與貢獻 120 億~160 億美元的上
緣。(3) 第二款 ASIC Humufish 維持預計將於 1Q28 步入量產，目前該平台部分
die 業已 tape out 完成，主要 die 也在 tape out 進行當中，先進封裝仍將優先採
用 Intel EMIB 。(4) 2027 年的營業利益率將>20%，反映營運槓桿效益提升。(5) 
第三代 ASIC(若順利應於 2029~2030 貢獻)則目前尚未定義最終規格。 
再者，公司亦表示第二個 ASIC 業務(搭載 NVLink Fusion) 預計 2028 年開始出貨
(前季法說僅表示尚與潛在客戶洽談中，本季已明確表示將於 2028 年出貨)，此為
新客戶(公司表示非中國客戶，故應表示該客戶非先前傳言的 Bytedance)。協助單
顆晶片設計與進入 NVLink Fusion 機櫃級設計的差異，在於幫客戶將晶片納入整
合進 Nvidia 的 AI 機櫃系統，需要增加高速互聯/相容性/記憶體架構/封裝協同設
計，以與 Nvidia 的交換器或網路系統偕同運作，反映聯發科能承接的工作範圍更
多，亦即接到單一客戶的商業價值應該更多。但因為此為聯發科首次導入 AI 機櫃
等級的業務，相關客戶與細項內容，均尚難知曉，故研究部本次財務預估尚未將此
估算在內。 
維持買進：考量 ASIC 業務貢獻優於預期，研究部上修獲利，預估聯發科 2026 年
營收 6874 億元(+15%YoY)，毛利率 45.87%，營業利率 16.9%，稅後獲利 1168
億元(+11%YoY)，稅後 EPS 72.8 元(原預估 70.5 元)，2027 年營收 1.17 兆元
(+71%YoY)， 毛利率43.77%， 營業利率21.5%， 稅後獲利2292 億元(+96%YoY)，

Document 126: 261029_daiwa_Win Semiconductors

Source PDF: all report pdf/261029_daiwa_Win Semiconductors.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: Following our results memo, we raise our forecasts and TP 
for Win Semiconductors (WinSemi), but lower our rating by one notch to 
Outperform (2) on valuation grounds. While its topline trajectory appears to 
be unfolding intact, its margin trajectory is running better than our 
expectations. This, together with management’s positive commentary 
across its key business lines of RF, infrastructure and optics, reinforces our 
thesis that 3 growth pillars will turn around WinSemi’s bottom line and 
optimise its structural profitability (Upgrading: profit optimisation round the 
corner, 24 July). We suggest accumulating only on pullbacks.

What’s the impact: Forecast revisions. We raise our 2027-28E EPS by 
2-11% for WinSemi to factor in its higher margin trajectory, primarily on 
better product mix geared towards the infrastructure business, followed by 
the ramp-up in optics for AI optical communications (OC). We note the 
surge in our 2026E EPS revision is a result of the company’s non-operating 
gains of TWD684m in 3Q26, mainly from its 23%-owned ITEQ (6213 TT, 
TWD778, Buy [1], covered by Sheng Cheng), which distorts WinSemi’s 
growth trajectory at the bottom line into 2027.

Transformation paying off. At its results call, management commented on 
resilient RF demand from iOS and for WiFi routers, strong demand for low 
Earth orbit (LEO) satellite communications, and a continued ramp in pin 
diode (PD) sales after the product entered mass-production from late-
2Q26. This should be followed by laser diode (LD), such as continuous 
wave (CW) and electro-absorption modulated laser (EML), though 
customer qualifications will take time. All the commentary reinforces our 
thesis that 3 growth pillars will help turn around WinSemi’s bottom line for 
profitability optimisation after its business transformation: 1) RF business 
transformation paying off with premiumisation across cellular and WiFi for 
differentiation, 2) high-margin infrastructure scaling up on a robust LEO 
build, and 3) AI OC ramping fast, with broadened offerings from peripheral 
chips (optical drivers and modulators) to core chips for PD and LD.

What we recommend: Given our forecast revisions, we lift our 12M TP for 
WinSemi to TWD620 (previous: TWD449), based on a higher 4-quarter 
forward PER target of 55x (previous: 50x), justified by its 2025-28E EPS 
CAGR of 54% (previous: 50%) from a PEG perspective. Yet we lower our 
rating by one notch to Outperform (2) on valuation grounds, with key 
downside risk of commodity cost hikes worsening consumer demand.

How we differ: Our 2026E EPS is 40% above consensus due to our 
inclusion of the non-operating gains in 3Q26, while our 2027E EPS is 9% 
above likely on our higher margin assumptions.

29 October 2026

Downgrading: profit optimisation on the go

➢ Raising our forecasts on a higher margin trajectory 
➢ Growth pillars paying off across infrastructure, optics and RF 
➢ Downgrading our rating to Outperform (2), with a higher TP of TWD620

Company profile 
Founded in 1999, Win Semiconductors Corp (WinSemi) is the world’s largest compound semiconductor 
foundry, focusing on gallium-arsenide (GaAs) foundry services for customers in both wireless and wire-
line communication markets, as well as infrastructure applications. It has a diverse technology portfolio 
of processes that supports microwave frequency requirements from 50MHz to 100GHz, with 
applications including smartphones, tablet PCs, cellular base-stations, very small aperture terminals 
(VSAT) and fibre optics. It has started penetrating into vertical cavity surface emitting laser (VCSEL) 
foundries since 2017 for consumer laser applications such as 3D laser sensors and cameras.

### Page 5

5

Win Semiconductors (3105 TT): 29 October 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 4 May 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
WinSemi's board of directors (BoD) has 10 members, including 3 independent directors,
representing 30% of total directors. Among the BoD, three of its non-independent directors are
also executive management.
Capital management 2
Since 2017, its dividend payout ratio has remained above 58%, except for 2024 when no
dividend was paid. Despite being the largest compound semiconductor foundry in the world,
WinSemi is continually expanding its capacity for future growth. Thus, we see the balance
between investment and dividend as appropriate.
Related party &
transaction 1 It did not have any meaningful related-party transactions in 2023.
S Supply chainmanagement 2
WinSemi We actively cooperate with local suppliers and improve their development capabilities,
reduce carbon emissions and create local employment opportunities. WinSemi also conducts on-
site audits of suppliers and annual assessment of suppliers on environmental protection and
safety & health management systems. In 2023, 100% of WinSemi's new suppliers signed the
"WIN Supplier Code of Conduct".'"
E Water & wastewatermanagement 1
Effluent that flows out of WinSemi’s facilities will first undergo pre-treatment in-house and then
treatment by the wastewater treatment plant at Hwaya Technology Park before discharge. The
plant has an acid and alkali wastewater recycling system and chemical mechanical polishing
wastewater recycling system for wastewater management.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1
WinSemi has established a hazardous substance-free management procedure and also
collaborates with its suppliers to produce products that are hazardous substance-free for
customers and consumers. WinSemi specifies in purchase contracts and order forms with its
suppliers that the provided materials may not contain hazardous substances as defined by
WinSemi.
For waste management, it aims to increase the recycling rate of waste solvents and reduce the
use of raw materials in production.
E GHG emissions 1
For reduction of energy consumption, it prioritises high-efficiency and low energy consumption
models when replacing equipment and has also established annual energy conservation
management objectives. WinSemi targets to reduce electricity consumption (kWh/6"e wafer) by
12% from 2018 to 458.58 and PFCs emission (ton CO2e/6"e wafer) by 50% from 2018 to 0.114
in 2030.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

Cloud Semis: Growing Cloud
Capex into 2027
Hyperscalers’ spending remains strong so far, with further
expansion signaled for 2027 amid FCF turning negative. Positive
read for cloud semis (Aspeed and Montage).
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Daniel Yen, CFA
Equity Analyst
Daniel.Yen@morganstanley.com                                                +886 2 2730-2863
Charlie Chan
Equity Analyst

Morgan Stanley appreciates your support in
the 2027 Extel Asia Research Survey. Voting
opens 10 November 2026.
Greater China Technology Semiconductors
Asia Pacific
Industry View Attractive
2027 capex growth is highlighted: The Big Four hyperscalers' combined C3Q capex
was US$185bn, +90% Y/Y. CY2026 guidance stands at approximately US$170bn for
Microsoft, US$195–205bn for Google, US$135–145bn for Meta, and US$220bn for
Amazon. Google and Amazon maintained guidance; Meta raised its spending floor

US$5bn. More importantly, Microsoft expects spending to accelerate in January–
June 2027, Google anticipates a significant increase in 2027, and Amazon expects
meaningfully higher spending. Meta also plans to keep investing aggressively.
Supply constraints remain the issue: Microsoft reported C3Q26 capex of US
$45.2bn and expects C4Q spending >US$50bn, with some capacity deliveries
shifting into 1H27. Google invested US$51.4bn and turned to third-party computing

late in C3Q to address supply constraints, with usage rising in C4Q. Meta invested
US$34.9bn, including finance lease principal payments; Amazon’s cash capex
reached US$75.9bn. Inference already contributes well over half of AWS AI revenue.
Debating monetization: Microsoft generated US$18.6bn of quarterly FCF and
expects positive FCF in FY2027. Google, Meta, and Amazon reported negative
quarterly FCF of US$9.1bn, US$2.1bn, and US$33.5bn, respectively. Google also

flagged depreciation, data center operating costs, and third-party computing
expenses as near-term cloud margin headwinds, alongside TPU inventory cash
requirements. Management remains focused on attractive long-term returns.
Cloud semis: Constructive read-through. Sustained server investment supports
Aspeed's and Montage's demand outlook; delayed capacity could shift revenue
between quarters, but it's just a timing issue. Approximately 75% of Microsoft’s C3Q

capex targeted short-lived assets, including CPUs, GPUs, and higher memory/storage
costs, up from two-thirds mentioned before. Google allocated ~65% to servers and
networking.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of
interest that could affect the objectivity of Morgan Stanley
Research. Investors should consider Morgan Stanley

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
2027 capex is highlighted: Microsoft, Google and Amazon flag higher spending;
Meta remains committed to aggressive investment.
Capacity constraints, not budget: Microsoft’s delivery delays and Google’s third-
party computing usage point to infrastructure bottlenecks.
Cloud semis: Constructive read-through so far. Sustained hyperscaler spending

provides a favorable backdrop, with capacity additions raising demand.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

### Page 7

GUC (3443.TW)
05 November 2026 Citi Research
7
GUC
Company description
Founded in 1998, Global Unichip (GUC) specializes in providing IC design
services, particularly in ASIC service. As a subsidiary of TSMC, which holds
34.8% of GUC, it offers a wide range of design services including spec-in and
SoC integration, physical implementation, 2.5D/3D packaging technologies,
mass production services, and advanced technologies such as high
bandwidth memory (HBM) and chip to chip interconnect IP .  With its

diversified global presence – Taiwan, North America, China, Europe, South
Korea, and Japan – the company offers services including mass production,
NRE, customer wafer verification projects, and IP licensing. Mass production
services provide customers with complete service, from back-end design
completion to wafer manufacturing, testing and management.
Investment strategy
We rate GUC stock as Buy/High Risk. We are seeing strong growth from its
CPU projects with two leading CSP customers. We also see strong order

potential from its EV clients. Long-term, we still believe back-end design
capability and 3DIC system integration will be critical to AI accelerators,
which would give better opportunities for TSMC 3DFabric partners with GUC
also being a beneficiary, in our view.
Valuation
Our target price for GUC of NT$8,000 (rounded) is based on 37x PER to the
average of our 2027 /28E EPS, slightly above its historical 3-year PER average
which we believe is justified by its strong earnings outlook supported by solid

demand from its AI projects with two US leading CSPs and growth upside
from its US EV clients. Our target price equates to 2027 /28E PB of 30x/14x.
Risks
Our quant risk rating system rates the GUC stock High Risk for its share-price
volatility. Downside risks that could mean the stock trades below our target
price include: 1) slower projects demand from US CSP customers, 2) lower-
than-expected NRE demand from less AI ASIC projects, and 3) delayed or less

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

Document 206: 35岁不会写代码？这波 AI 红利，可能就轮到你了

Source PDF: all report pdf/35岁不会写代码？这波 AI 红利，可能就轮到你了.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-05
Pages: 8
Status: ok

### Page 1

最缺的不是更强的  AI 模型，⽽是既懂业务、⼜能把  AI 真正⽤起来的⼈。
摘要：
今年以来，全球科技公司已宣布裁员近  20 万⼈。与此同时，有⼀个  AI 岗位的招聘需求却在爆
发：美国的招聘⼴告三年涨了  30 多倍，薪资中位数约  19.5 万美元；国内相关职位的发布量⼀
年涨了  21 倍。
OpenAI 、 Anthropic 、微软、亚⻢逊、⾕歌，⼏家巨头前后砸下近百亿美元，有的开新公司，有
的建新部⻔，抢的都是这类⼈才。最反常识的是：这个岗位最稀缺的本事，恰恰不是写代码。
Anthropic ：培养  1 万个 “ 把  AI 送进企业的⼈ ”
10 ⽉  2 ⽇， Claude 的开发商  Anthropic 宣布投⼊  1 亿美元成⽴ “Claude 前沿学院 ” （ Claude
Frontier Academy ），⽬标是在  2027 年底前培养  1 万名 “ 前沿部署⼯程师 ” 。
⾸批学员是从合作伙伴中选出的在职⼯程师，来⾃埃森哲、摩根⼠丹利、诺和诺德和⼏家顶级
咨询公司。培训分两段：先是  4 天线下集训，从⼀次 “ 模拟企业部署 ” 开始；考核通过后，学员回
到⾃⼰公司，带⼀个真实项⽬做  12 周实战。⾸批认证预计在  2027 年初完成。
在  Anthropic 看来，企业落地  AI 的瓶颈不在模型，⽽在于缺少能推动变⾰的⼈。负责这个项⽬
的⾼管  Steve Corfield 说，客⼾现在缺的，是既懂业务怎么运转、⼜能亲⼿把  AI 从⼀个想法做
成上线系统的⼈。
这只是今年  AI 巨头抢⼈⼤战中的最新⼀例。

砸下百亿美元，巨头们都在抢同⼀种⼈才
今年  5 ⽉， OpenAI 成⽴了专⻔帮企业落地  AI 的 “OpenAI 部署公司 ” ，由  TPG 领投，⾸轮融资
超过  40 亿美元。同⽉， Anthropic 与⿊⽯、 Hellman & Friedman 、⾼盛合资成⽴了⼀家  AI 服
务公司，各⽅初始投资约  15 亿美元。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

6 ⽉底到  7 ⽉初，亚⻢逊  AWS 和微软先后出⼿： AWS 投⼊  10 亿美元，组建⾃⼰的部署⼯程
团队；微软投⼊  25 亿美元成⽴新部⻔，把约  6000 名⼯程专家派驻到客⼾企业。 9 ⽉，⾕歌云
和埃森哲成⽴联合业务部⻔，计划培训最多  1000 名埃森哲⼯程师，专⻔派驻客⼾现场。
过去这些公司⽐的是谁的模型更强、参数更⼤、跑分更⾼，如今却不约⽽同地把钱砸向同⼀个
⽅向：派⼈进客⼾公司，把  AI 真正⽤起来。
这类⼈才在业内有个统⼀的叫法：FDE （ Forward Deployed Engineer ），中⽂常译作“ 前沿部
署⼯程师 ”。
招聘量三年猛涨 30 倍，⼤⼚开出百万年包
熟悉美股的读者，对这个词的源头不会陌⽣。⼗多年前， Palantir （帕兰泰尔）就系统化地推⾏
了这种做法：⼯程师不坐在总部写通⽤产品，⽽是常驻客⼾现场，和客⼾⽤同⼀套数据、扛同
⼀个业务指标，把模糊的需求、分散的数据⼀点点变成能⽤的系统。 Palantir CEO Alex Karp 有

⼀个被业内反复引⽤的判断：⼤模型当然重要，但⾄少在未来⼏年，价值在于落地实施。
如今，这套做法正在变成⼀个热⻔职业。据劳动⼒市场分析机构  Lightcast 统计， 2023 年全美
FDE 招聘⼴告不到  200 条， 2026 年前九个⽉已超过  7300 条，三年涨了  30 多倍。⼴告标出的
薪资中位数约  19.5 万美元，是全美招聘⼴告薪资中位数的三倍多。发布  FDE ⼴告最多的雇主
中，德勤、埃森哲等咨询公司排在⾕歌等科技巨头之前。可⻅  FDE 早已不只是  AI 公司的技术
岗，凡是要把  AI ⽤进业务的地⽅，都需要这样的⼈。
FDE 这股⻛已经刮到国内。脉脉平台数据显⽰， 2026 年上半年，平台上  FDE 相关职位的发布
量同⽐飙升  21 倍。字节跳动的 “ ⾖包  AI ⼤模型  FDE” 年包最⾼约  105 万元，蚂蚁数科、智谱等
也开出了  90 万元以上的年包。但即便开出⾼薪，合适的⼈依然难招：猎聘⼤数据研究院统计，
2025 年  9 ⽉⾄  2026 年  8 ⽉，国内  FDE ⼈才的供需⽐只有  0.28 ，相当于将近  4 个岗位抢  1 个

⼈。
这个缺⼝还在被政策放⼤。⼯信部  8 ⽉底印发通知，⿎励  AI 应⽤服务商搭建  FDE 团队、扎根
⽤⼾现场；武汉光⾕提出  3 年培育  1000 名  FDE ，上海提出要建⽴超千⼈规模的  FDE ⼯程师储
备库。企业、服务商、地⽅政府都在要⼈，合格的  FDE 远远不够⽤。
AI 落地难在业务，最缺的恰恰是懂业务的⼈
看到这⾥，很多⼈会想：这是年轻程序员的机会，我不写代码，也过了转⾏的年纪。但不妨看
看  Anthropic 怎么描述⼀个好的  FDE ：熟悉  Agent 系统、擅⻓解决问题、善于沟通、有⼯程经
验。这四条⾥，有⼀半在技术之外。
AI 落地最难的部分，本来就不在代码。过去两年，⼏乎每家企业都买过  AI 产品、做过试点，真
正跑进⽇常业务的却少得多。标普全球的调查显⽰，放弃⼤部分  AI 项⽬的企业，⼀年内从  17%
涨到了  42% ； IDC 和联想  2025 年的报告更直接： 88% 的  AI 概念验证项⽬，最后都没能投⼊
⽣产。
演⽰时⼀切顺利，⼀进真实业务，就撞上脏数据、⽼系统、权限审批、员⼯习惯，还有那个绕

不开的问题：出了错谁负责。这也是  FDE 和驻场外包的区别：外包是甲⽅说什么就做什么；
FDE 要先判断这件事该不该⽤  AI 、⽤哪个模型、花多少钱、怎么证明有效，再动⼿上线。
所以  FDE 真正值钱的那⼀半能⼒，是懂业务。⼀份合同哪⼏条要紧，⼀张报表哪个数字会出
事，⼀个审批流程卡在哪⼀步，哪件事交给机器会让⽼板睡不着觉，这些  AI 替代不了，在⾏业
⾥⼲了⼗⼏、⼆⼗年的⼈反⽽最清楚。

### Page 3

腾讯云副总裁张渝前不久的⼀句话，戳中了很多企业的痛处：过去花⼤价钱请外部团队做项
⽬， " 项⽬做完，团队撤场，能⼒也跟着带⾛了 " 。越来越多企业意识到，与其等外部团队来了⼜
⾛，不如让⾃⼰懂业务的⼈⻓出这项能⼒。
如果你已经在⾏业⾥深耕多年，那么你缺的从来不是经验，⽽是把经验交给  AI 去执⾏的那⼀
步：知道今天的  AI 能做什么、做不了什么，该怎么搭、要花多少钱、出了错怎么兜底。我们这
次的训练营，就是为这⼀步准备的。
两天⾛完  AI 落地最后⼀公⾥
华尔街⻅闻⾸期「 FDE 实战训练营」将于  11 ⽉  14 ⽇⾄  15 ⽇在上海开班。这是⼀个线下实战
营，限量  50 席，在两天时间⾥，带你亲⼿搭出⾃⼰的第⼀个  AI Agent ，⾛完⼀遍从选模型、算
成本到评测上线的全流程。
先说说  Agent （智能体）是什么。很多⼈对  AI 的印象还停留在 “ 问⼀句答⼀句 ” 的聊天上，但
Agent 不⼀样：你交给它⼀件事，它会⾃⼰查资料、打开软件、读写⽂件、交出结果，做错了还
能⾃⼰返⼯。巨头们派  FDE 进企业，部署的正是这种技术。
两天训练营共  12 个模块，从模型怎么选、价格怎么算，⼀直讲到评测、成本优化和安全上线，
都是⼤⼚把  AI 做上线时真正在⽤的⽅法。主讲讲师  Right 曾任腾讯⾼级算法⼯程师，主导过微
信核⼼  AI Agent 项⽬的算法设计和规模化落地，在  Agent 系统设计、模型训练、 Harness 架构
⽅向有深厚积累。他还在  NeurIPS 、 ACL 等顶级会议发表过多篇  Agent ⽅向论⽂，相关⼯作被
Google DeepMind 引⽤。
课程适合谁
企业主和管理者：想弄清  AI 到底能替公司做哪些事、每件事花多少钱、⻛险在哪⾥
⾦融、法务、财务、运营、咨询等专业⼈⼠：⼿上有⼤量重复⼯作，想亲⼿做⼀个⼯具交给
AI
想在  AI 时代给⾃⼰多留⼀项硬技能的⼈

三⼗年前，先学会⽤  Excel 的那批⼈，在后来的⼆⼗年⾥少熬了⽆数个夜。
今天巨头们花百亿美元在抢懂业务、⼜会⽤  AI 的⼈，⽽你已经有了⼀半。剩下那⼀半，就在这
两天⾥补⻬。

營收走勢圖

研究員/莊佳蓉 EVA8894122@uni-psg.com

雖考量 VR 整體遞延加上中系手機客戶拉貨趨緩，研究部微幅下修 27
年預估，但仍然維持富世達買進投資建議，主係：(1)3Q26 伺服器營收
占比大幅提升至 81%，帶動毛利率達 38.0%，創歷史新高並優於預期；
(2)QD 月產能已擴充至 450 萬顆 （+50%） ， 且2027 年持續擴產 ；(3)2027
年受惠 VR 放量，加上CSP ASIC 專案陸續貢獻，營運成長性高；(4)目
前評價已回落至 17.7 倍，位於低檔。綜上所述，研究部下修 2027 年
營收至 295 億元 （原估316 億元） ，EPS 下修至 121.74 元 （原估124.48
元），給予 24 倍本益比，目標價下調至 2,920 元。

重點摘要： 
1. 產品組合大幅優化，3Q26毛利率躍升至38.0% 
3Q26營收45億元，季增20.4%，年增26.3%，符合預期。其中：
(1)3Q成長主要來自伺服器QD，季增將近8成，主因GB300持續
出貨，加上VR200小量出貨；(2)中系手機部分，受記憶體漲價
及缺貨影響，終端拉貨力道疲弱，單季營收季減51%；(3)滑軌
部分，因通用伺服器記憶體缺料影響，季減將近4成。整體而
言，因QD營收大幅提升，伺服器營收占比由2Q的55%大幅提升
至3Q的81%，產品組合轉佳，故毛利率達38.0%（原估37.0%），
季增4.0ppts，優於預期。營益率32.4%（原估32.4%） ，季增
4.5ppts，符合預期；EPS達18.11元（原估17.57元） ，優於預
期。

2. 4Q26營收微幅下修，惟毛利率續創新高 
展望4Q26，考量中系手機客戶雖為旺季， 但受缺料及漲價影響 ，
拉貨態度偏保守。在VR部分，根據供應鏈調查，若以L11機櫃
組裝端來看，4Q26~1Q27僅小量出貨，真正放量須至2Q27，時
程遞延。但若以零組件端看，液冷QD屬於偏L6端的產品，出貨
時程較早，4Q已開始出貨。但考量放量時程及手機影響，研究
部微幅下修4Q26營收至51億元 （原估53億元） ，季增12.7%，年
增27.6%。毛利率部分，考量伺服器占比可望維持在高檔，上
修毛利率至38.6%（原估37.3%） ，季增0.6ppts；EPS微幅下修
至20.42元（原估20.89元）。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

3. NV、ASIC雙引擎並進 
基本面仍維持正向看待，QD成長動能明確：(1)NV VR 4Q逐漸
出貨，動能延續至 27年；下一代Ultra設計將導入更高流量
ZQD(單價翻倍)， 促進價值提升；(2)AWS T3已配合客戶時程出
貨，新世代平台水冷滲透率進一步提升；(3)其他ASIC專案陸
續進入量產，本次新增MSFT Maia QD，預估單櫃約576顆(32顆
*18層)，Google TPU、Meta也陸續出貨。產能方面，QD月產能
已擴充至450萬顆（+50%），2027年持續擴產，預期提升至650
萬顆，足以支撐後續訂單需求。

4. VR放量時程遞延，保守下修2027年營收 
然而 ，研究部仍考量以下因素 ， 保守微幅下修2027年營收預估 ：
(1)NV平台部分，考量VR延後至2Q27放量，恐連帶影響Rubin 
Ultra，且目前機櫃架構亦尚未定案，ZQD用量存在變數；(2)
中系手機客戶整體拉貨動能偏弱， 研究部預估明年衰退，故些
微下修27年預估。

5. 目前評價已回檔，潛在空間浮現，維持買進建議 
綜上所述 ， 研究部下修2026年營收至175億元 （原估178億元） ，
年增41.0%，惟毛利率上修至36.0%（原估35.4%），EPS上修至
64.30元 （原估64.20元）。同步下修2027年營收至295億元 （原
估316億元），EPS微幅下修至121.74元 （原估124.48元） 。目前
評價已回落至17.7倍，位於低檔，潛在空間已浮現，給予24倍
本益比，目標價由3,115元調整至2,920元，維持買進建議。

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

附 錄 二 ： 免 責 宣 言 
© 2010 統 一 投 資 顧 問 股 份 有 限 公 司 版 權 所 有 。 本 公 司 提 供 之 報 告 內 容 係 根 據 本 公
司 認 可 之 資 料來 源 ， 並 基 於 特 定 日 期 所 做 之 判 斷 ， 但 不 保 證 其 完 整 性 或 正 確 性 ， 報
告 中 所 有 的 意 見 及 預 估 ， 如 有 變 更 恕 不 另 行 通 知 。

本 研 究 報 告 所 載 之 投 資 資 訊 ， 僅 提 供 客 戶 做 為 一 般 投 資 參 考 ， 並 非 針 對 特 定 對 象 提
供 專 屬 之 投 資 建 議 。 文 中 所 載 資 訊 或 任 何 意 見 ， 不 構 成 任 何 買 賣 有 價 證 券 或 其 他 投
資 標 的 之 要 約 、 宣 傳 或 引 誘 等 事 項 。 對 於 本 投 資 報 告 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 任 何 證 券 、 投
資 標 的 ， 或 文 中 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 投 資 策 略 ， 投 資 人 應 就 其 是 否 適 合 本 身 財 務 狀 況 與
投 資 條 件 ， 進 一 步 諮 詢 財 務 顧 問 的 意 見 。 本 投 資 報 告 之 內 容 取 材 自 據 信 為 可 靠 之 資
料 來 源 ， 但 概 不 以 明 示 或 默 示 的 方 式 ， 對 資 料 之 準 確 性 、 完 整 性 或 正 確 性 作 出 任 何
陳 述 或 保 證 。 本 投 資 報 告 載 述 意 見 進 行 更 改 與 撤 回 並 不 另 行 通 知 。 本 投 資 報 告 並 非
（且不應 解 釋 為 ）在 任 何 司 法 管 轄 區 內 ， 任 何 非 依 法 從 事 證 券 經 紀 或 交 易 之 人 士 或
公 司 ， 為 於 該 管 轄 區 內 從 事 證 券 經 紀 或 交 易 之 遊 說 。 本 投 資 報 告 內 容 屬 統 一 投 顧 之
著 作 權 ， 嚴 禁 抄 襲 與 仿 造 。

3Q26 Review: 4 분기 실적 반등 기대감 Up  
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 600,000원(유지) 
 현재주가 292,500원(10/29)

 동사의 3분기 실적은 매출액 45.1조원(-3.4%yoy), 영업이익 2.68조원
(+5.5%yoy, OPM 5.9%), 지배이익 1.7조원(-24.6%yoy)을 기록하며 시장 
컨센서스를 상회. 도매 판매 감소, 원가 부담 증가 영향 있었으나 판매보증
충당금 축소와 원가 절감 활동을 통해 수익성 방어 
 실적 증감 요인: 영업이익 증감 요인은 물량 -1.11조원, 믹스/인센티브 -
0.57조원, 금융 -0.11조원, 환율 +1.28조원, 기타 +0.64조원 등. 원자재 
가격 상승으로 5천억원의 비용 증가 요인 있었으나 위기 대응 원가 절감 
활동으로 대부분 상쇄할 수 있었음. 전분기 대비 기말환율 급락함에 따라 
판매보증충당금 지난 해 1.26조원에서 340억원으로 약 1.23조원 감소. 판
보충 효과는 일회성에 그치겠지만 원가 절감 효과 컸던 점 긍정적  
 금융 부문: 금융부문 매출액은 전년비 2.8% 증가했지만 영업이익은 26% 
급감. 장기 금리 상승에 따른 이자 비용 증가와 기업금융 충당금 적립, 중고
차 관련 비용 확대가 수익성 악화의 주요 원인. 중고차 관련 비용은 EV 리
스 반납 차량의 매각 손실 확대에 기인한 것 
 4분기 전망: 동사는 국내 공장 정상화와 아반떼, 투싼, GV90, GV80 HEV 
등 주요 신차 효과를 통해 4분기 생산 확대와 주요 수출 시장 공급 회복이 
가능할 것으로 전망하였음. 원가 부담은 지속되나 물량 감소에 따른 영향에
서는 벗어날 수 있을 것. 원자재 가격 상승에 대응해 원가절감 프로그램 확
대와 신차 출시를 통한 인센티브 정상화를 통해 수익성 개선 지속할 계획 
 연간 가이던스 하향 조정: 글로벌 도매 판매 목표를 기존 416만대에서 
400만대로 하향 조정. 매출 성장률은 +1~2%, OPM 가이던스는 기존 
6.3~7.3%에서 5.5~6.5%로 하향 조정. 자동차 부문 투자 계획은 17.8조
원에서 16.0조원으로 축소. 핵심 R&D 투자는 유지하되 Capex 집행 시기 
및 우선 순위 조정할 계획 
 4분기 실적 반등 전망: 4분기 주요 신차(투싼, 아반떼, GV80 HEV, GV90) 
출시 본격화로 3분기까지의 부진을 만회할 수 있을 것으로 기대. 원가 부담 
은 비용 절감 활동을 통해 충분히 만회가 가능한 것으로 3분기 실적을 통
해 확인됨. 본격적인 주가 반등을 위해서는 판매 회복 이상의 주가 트리거 
필요할 것으로 보이나, 4분기 반전의 실마리가 나타나고 있다는 점에 주목. 
동사에 대한 투자의견 매수와 목표주가 60만원을 유지함

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

### Page 1

2026.10.30   
한화에어로스페이스  
(012450)

3Q26: 그래도 믿는다  Company Brief

3분기 매출 6조 943억원/영업이익 4,520억원 
한화에어로스페이스의 3분기 매출은 6조 943억원/영업이익 4,520억원(OPM 7.4%)로 
컨센서스와 당사 추정치를 모두 대폭 하회하는 실적을 기록하였다. 앞서 실적을 발표한 
한화오션의 실적 쇼크를 감안하더라도, 주력사업인 지상방산 부문의 실적이 매출 1조 
2,455억원/영업이익 217억원(1.7%)로 지난 분기(2조 1,075억원/ 5,330억원/ 25.3%) 
는 물론 전년동기(2조 1,098억원/5,725억원/27.1%)보다도 현저히 감소하였다. 
일회성 요인은 대전사업장 폭발사고에 따른 복구비용 등 300억원, 일부 국내사업 
충당금 160억원 및 환율하락에 따른 효과 등이 있었으나 역시 이를 감안해도 내수와 
수출 모두 실질 이익률은 5%미만으로 추정된다. 지상방산 분기 매출은 1분기(1조 
2,211억)보다 오히려 소폭 증가하였으나 영업이익이 1분기 2,087억원에서 
217억원으로 대폭 감소한 점에 대하여 회사는 프로젝트별 믹스 구성에 따른 낮은 
이익률의 인도분이 반영되었다고 설명하고 있으나, 그럼에도 불구하고 수출과 내수 
이익률 모두 BEP에 가까웠다는 점은 쉽게 이해가 가지는 않는다. 다만 방산기업의 
특성상 대전사업장 사고에 따른 실제 납기 영향 등 회사가 공개할 수 없는 사정이 
있었을 것으로 추측할 뿐이다. 대전사업장은 현재 복구작업이 진행 중이며 27년 
상반기 중에는 가동 정상화할 것으로 예상된다.  
긍정적인 소식도 있다. 폴란드향 천무의 연간 인도대수가 기존 가이던스인 40대에서 
60대로 증가하였으며, 이를 포함하여 3분기에 인도되지 않은 물량의 대부분이 
4분기에 집중될 예정으로, 올해의 4분기 실적 개선폭은 통상적으로 볼 수 있는 
4분기의 계절적 집중도보다 더 커질 것으로 예상한다. 방산기업의 실적은 인도일정에 
따라 크게 변할 수 있는 분기 실적보다 연간 합산이 더 중요하다는 점을 한 번 더 
믿고, 4분기를 기다리는 게 좋겠다.

투자의견 BUY 유지, 목표주가 1,200,000원 하향  
투자의견 BUY를 유지하지만, SOTP기반 목표주가는 기존 1,490,000원에서 
1,200,000원으로 19.5% 하향한다. 비영업가치 중 자회사 한화시스템과 한화오션, 
쎄트렉아이의 지분가치 하락(합산 8.7조원→7.1조원), 그리고 지난 분기 대비 늘어난 
별도 순차입금(3.25조→4.95조원)을 반영하였으며 최근 방산섹터 전반의 밸류 하락을 
감안하여 영업가치 중 지상방산+항공우주의 Target P/E를 기존 35배에서 30배로 
조정하였다. 
3분기는 여러모로 악재가 겹쳤다. 주식시장 전반의 하락에 더해 방산섹터 수주 지연에 
따른 디레이팅, 그리고 납기 지연에 따른 실적 하락 등 나올 수 있는 악재는 다 
나왔다고도 볼 수 있다. 그러나 뒤집어보면 지연중인 수주는 시간의 문제일 뿐 가시화 
가능성이 매우 높으며, 실적 또한 진행률 인식이 아닌 인도기준 인식에 따른 일시적 
현상으로 4분기 이후 복구될 가능성이 크다. 펀더멘털의 유의미한 변화는 없다고 볼 
수 있으며 밸류에이션 매력도가 증가한 현 시점에서 추가 매수를 고려할 때다. 다만, 
대전사업장 사고에 따른 당국의 제재조치가 아직 확정되지 않았음은 잔여 리스크 
요인이다. 동종사의 신규계약 입찰제한 제재조치가 주가에 상당한 영향을 미쳤음을 
고려하면 한화에어로스페이스 또한 제재조치 여부를 지켜본 후 투자할 것을 권한다.

Compliance notice 
당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
 당 연구원은 한화에어로스페이스(012450)의 일부 비용을 지원받아 한화에어로스페이스 공장 투어(2026년 10월 1-2)를 다녀왔습니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

Document 272: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지HD현대중공업(329180) 업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지HD현대중공업(329180) 업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지업종 내 여전히 매력적인 선택지 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-15
Pages: 5
Status: ok

### Page 1

HD현대중공업의 3Q26 연결 실적은 매출액 5조 8,602억원(yoy +33%, qoq -7%), 
영업이익 9,161억원(yoy +64%, qoq -12%, OPM 15.6%)으로 시장 컨센서스를 하회할 
전망이다. 동사의 3Q26 조업일수는 여름휴가, 추석연휴 및 중대재해 등 영향으로 
qoq -21% 감소했으며 환율 역시 qoq -84원 감소한 영향을 감안해 전분기 대비 
실적을 보수적으로 추정했다. 그럼에도 해양 부문 Trion FPU발 C/O에 따른 일회성 
환입이 예상됨에 따라 일부 이익 상쇄가 가능할 것으로 예상된다. 한편, 동사의 
임단협 부결로 당분기 추가적인 인건비 부담은 제한적일 것으로 판단되며 잠수함 
화재 사고 관련 비용 역시 4Q 이후 반영될 전망이다.

동사는 급격히 커져가는 AIDC향 전력수요 및 발전용 엔진 수주에 대응하고자 9/10 
총 4GW 규모의 4행정 중속엔진 캐파 증설을 위한 신규 투자를 공시헸다. 동사의 
기존 DC향 4행정 중속엔진 수주 단가를 고려 시 GW당 약 1조원의 매출 창출이 
가능할 전망이며 2행정 및 선박용 4행정 엔진 대비 높은 수익성이 예상되는 바 
생산이 본격화될 28년부터 동사 실적 성장성의 핵심 동인으로 작용할 전망이다.

동사에 대해 투자의견 BUY를 유지하며 업종 밸류에이션 디레이팅 및 시장 프리미엄 
조정에 따른 Target Multipe 변동으로 목표주가를 70만원으로 하향 조정한다. 상선 
시장 수주 경쟁 심화로 업종 밸류 조정이 이루어지고 있으나 동사는 엔진 기반 장기 
성장성으로 Peer 대비 상대적 매력도 확보 및 주가 방어가 가능하다는 판단이다.

Document 293: AI股暴跌蔓延亚洲，高净值客户面临追加保证金

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI股暴跌蔓延亚洲，高净值客户面临追加保证金.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-06
Pages: 2
Status: ok

### Page 1

⾹港⾼净值客⼾被迫折价接收结构性票据标的股票；⾼盛估算，亚洲选股基⾦上⽉平均亏
损 9.4% 。基⾦经理 Chris McGuire 直⾔：这是互联⽹泡沫以来最极端⾏情，本质是集中仓
位的连锁爆仓，⽽⾮基本⾯重估。
摘要：
美国科技股隔夜急挫，冲击波迅速席卷亚洲市场，对冲基⾦与⾼净值投资者⾸当其冲。
据彭博报道，周五（ 11 ⽉ 6 ⽇），亚洲各地经纪商与基⾦经理被恐慌客⼾的电话淹没， AI 相关仓
位的集中抛售引发连锁反应，部分市场触发熔断机制。据知情⼈⼠透露，持有杠杆头⼨的富裕
客⼾正⾯临追加保证⾦的压⼒。
MSCI 亚太指数周五盘中⼀度下跌 5.3%，此前纳斯达克 100 指数周四单⽇蒸发创纪录的 2.3 万亿美
元市值。韩国综合股价指数（ Kospi ）跌幅⼀度⾼达 8.8% ，触发熔断机制。此轮抛售的背景是，
⺠主党在国会两院取得多数席位后，市场预期其将推动更严格的 AI 监管并放缓数据中⼼建设。
对冲基⾦损失惨重，亚洲股票多头全线受压
此轮 AI 股抛售已在对冲基⾦⾏业造成⼤范围亏损。据彭博报道， ExodusPoint Capital

Management 与 Millennium Management 等⼤型机构均录得损失，尽管部分基⾦从波动中获益。
亚洲市场的整体图景尤为黯淡。据报道， Valliance Asset Management 在致投资者的更新函中
披露，其多空策略主基⾦ 10 ⽉下跌 6.7% 。
⾼盛集团旗下⼤宗经纪业务估算，亚洲股票选股型基⾦上⽉平均亏损 9.4%，将今年以来的累计
涨幅压缩⾄ 14% 。
杠杆头⼨承压，经纪商紧急应对
报道称，⾹港⼀位经纪商表⽰，散⼾连夜涌⼊，向公司提出⼤量咨询，员⼯被迫加班以安抚客
⼾情绪并讨论⻛险管理策略。他坦⾔，对于客⼾⽽⾔，唯⼀的建议就是 “ 尽快出逃 ” ，并要求保证
⾦交易者尽快降低杠杆敞⼝。
不过，该经纪商也指出，由于公司数周前已开始要求客⼾为杠杆科技仓位补充抵押品，⼤规模
强制平仓的⻛险有所降低。
在⽇本，住友三井信托资产管理公司⾸席策略师 Hiroyuki Ueno 表⽰， “ 我们正在应接不暇地处理
有关 AI 抛售的各类询问，卖⽅客⼾正在努⼒向其客⼾解释究竟发⽣了什么。 ” 他同时指出，短期投

资者可以迅速作出反应，但⼤型机构基⾦⾯临的抉择则更为复杂。
对于亚洲众多投资者⽽⾔，当前的核⼼问题已不再是 AI 估值是否需要重置，⽽是此轮抛售究竟
还会延续多久。
T&D Asset Management Co. ⾸席策略师兼基⾦经理 Hiroshi Namioka 表⽰， “ 从我们团队早会的
感受来看，市场正在以⾮理性的⽅式运⾏。我不会说这是彻底的恐慌，但投资者确实很难看到
上⾏空间。 ”

市场参与者还指出，期权相关资⾦流在关键技术位失守后进⼀步放⼤了波动。⾹港独⽴分析
师、对冲基⾦顾问 Travis Lundy 表⽰，
美国市场同步承压， AI 基⾦遭重创
此轮抛售同样重创美国市场的 AI 相关基⾦。华尔街⻅闻⽂章写道， 170 亿美元基⾦ Walleye
Capital 单⽉亏损 5.9% ，被迫裁撤股票多空团队并返还投资者资⾦。系统性对冲基⾦ Engineers
Gate 亦宣布裁撤旗下⼤部分 "Synthesis" 股票团队。根源在于 AI 相关股票仓位⾼度集中，对冲基
⾦群体两周内抛售 200 亿美元科技股。 Millennium 、 Marshall Wace 等多家巨头同步受损。
与此同时，据彭博报道， Rob Pohly 旗下的 Samlyn Capital 已在削减⼏乎所有板块敞⼝（仅保留

全球⾦融股）后，开始裁减⾼级分析师；专注 AI 投资的对冲基⾦ Value Aligned Research
Advisors 上⽉暴跌 33% 。
从业者对此轮⾏情的严峻程度给出了直接评价。 1991 年便开始在芝加哥期权交易所（ CBOT ）
从事期权交易、现任 Eagle's View Capital Management ⽇本可转债套利基⾦经理的 Chris
McGuire 表⽰，“ 这⼏乎是我⾃互联⽹泡沫以来所⻅过的最极端⾏情。持仓集中于相同股票或板
块的股票持有者的集中平仓，制造了我们很⻓时间以来从未⻅过的单向动能。 ”他表⽰，其基⾦
本⽉估计获利 2.5% 或以上。
“ 这看起来更像是仓位爆仓，⽽⾮对未来的根本性重新评估。对部分基⾦⽽⾔，糟糕的 10 ⽉似乎
触发了 11 ⽉初的平仓潮，并形成了连锁效应。 ”

[Nhận định xu hướng] 
VN-Index có thể hồi kỹ thuật từ cạnh dưới tam giác, song việc chớm thủng hỗ trợ 
1.710 điểm và số mã còn giữ xu hướng tăng từ đáy tháng 7 suy gi ảm khiến xu 
hướng ngắn hạn trở nên trung tính.

### Page 2

2

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -29.18 điểm tương ứng -1.68%, với thanh khoản suy 
giảm. Dầu khí, Bảo hiểm và Dịch vụ phân phối là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt nhất 
trong tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -3,013 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở các mã MCH, VIC và FPT; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng ở mức 
887 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều bán ròng -2,021 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua và đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -4,384 hợp đồng. Tự doanh đảo chiều bán ròng -
4,275 hợp đồng và đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -7,815 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam tiếp tục điều chỉnh tuần thứ tư liên tiếp, khi VN-Index lùi về đóng cửa 1.706 
điểm - giảm khoảng 1,7% so với tuần trước và đang dao động quanh vùng hỗ trợ tâm lý 
1.700 điểm, cũng là vùng đáy cũ của tháng 8. Thanh khoản thị trường tiếp tục suy yếu, 
phản ánh tâm lý thận trọng cao độ trong bối cảnh thị trường thiếu vắng thông tin hỗ trợ 
trong nước, trong khi các yếu tố bên ngoài như xung đột tại Iran, và chi phí vốn toàn cầu 
chưa hạ nhiệt tiếp tục đè nặng tâm lý nhà đầu tư. Khối ngoại duy trì chuỗi bán ròng kéo 
dài nhiều phiên liên tiếp, nối dài đà rút vốn đã lên tới xấp xỉ 95 nghìn tỷ đồng từ đầu năm. 
Trong một, hai tuần tới, mùa báo cáo kết quả kinh doanh quý 3 được kỳ vọng là chất xúc 
tác giúp thị trường giao dịch cân bằng trở lại.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX tiếp tục hình thành mẫu nến giảm điểm tiêu cực trên đồ thị tuần, đánh dấu tuần 
giảm thứ 4 liên tiếp, cho thấy áp lực phân phối vẫn đang có phần lấn át. Trên khung ngày, 
tuy chỉ số đã có nỗ lực phục hồi trở lại trong 3 phiên đan xen, đặc biệt là phản ứng trong 
phiên cuối tuần khi chạm trendline trung hạn, nhưng việc chỉ có thể hình thành các mẫu 
nến "spinning" hàm ý đà tích lũy chưa thực sự thuyết phục. Trong một vài phiên kế tiếp, 
VNINDEX có thể xuất hiện nhịp hồi kỹ thuật từ vùng hỗ trợ, tương ứng với cạnh dưới tam 
giác, nhưng sau khi chớm phá vùng hỗ trợ quanh 1710 và hiện chỉ còn một số ít các mã giữ 
được xu hướng tăng tính từ đáy tháng 7, xu hướng ngắn hạn đang trở nên khá trung tính.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên)

Hỗ trợ gần: 1680 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1630 (+-15)

### Page 4

4

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

2 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
Oil Price Dynamics Remain Key to the Outlook
Chief Investment Office, Macro Strategy Team
Inflation, interest rates and equity market dynamics have changed materially since mid-
August, as widening attacks on Middle East and Russian crude oil and refining capacity
worsened supply and price conditions. Indeed, with the oil market already strained, an
early-September attack on a major Saudi Arabian export pipeline sharply raised concerns

about a protracted and deepening oil crisis. While Middle East crude exports eventually
managed to significantly rebound, increased security risks and freight costs raised
physical crude prices.
Against this backdrop, the spot Brent price surged from $92/barrel in mid-August
back into a $115 to $135/barrel price range until recently. Reflecting unusual market
tightness, other North Sea oil grades were recently trading even higher, up to $160/
barrel, according to the International Energy Agency (IEA). Even as it cooled from $135

to $115/barrel between October 2 and October 20, spot Brent is still almost double
year-ago levels, and so are many diesel prices. The AAA average U.S. retail diesel price
recently hit a new record and was 74% higher year-over-year (YoY) as of October 20.
With insufficient regional supply, the New York Harbor wholesale diesel price is 130%
higher from a year ago, according to the Energy Information Administration (EIA).
Indeed, sharply higher oil prices and a growing diesel shortage have put the global diesel

market under extreme strain. The U.S., for example, has little distillate cushion and
limited spare refining capacity. The distillate inventory (diesel and fuel oil) has decreased
from 37 days of demand coverage in January to just 26 days by early October, according
to the EIA. This 30% drop brought the supply buffer to the bottom of its historical
range. With limited refining capacity and reliance on imports for about 10% of regional
consumption, East Coast distillate inventories are the lowest since 1990, for example,

and are expected to remain below their 5-year average into late 2027, according to EIA
data. More broadly, according to the IEA’s October Oil Market Report (OMR), “oil market
balances are forecast to remain in deficit through the end of the year, with constrained
middle distillate supplies likely to persist well into 2027.”
Thus, although wage growth/labor demand do not appear inflationary, the oil crisis
risks continuing to feed “core” inflation. According to the Federal Reserve’s Beige Book 1

covering precisely the mid-August to early-October period of heightened oil-market
turmoil, “higher oil prices permeated all District reports,” with firms noting that fuel
surcharges were passing on increased costs throughout the supply chain. The 22% rise
in airfares over the past year alone contributed around 0.2 percentage point (pp) to the
2.6% 12-month increase in the “core” consumer price index (CPI) for September. Aside
from transportation costs effects, the spike in diesel prices also portends high food

prices with a few-month lag given planting/harvesting cost considerations.
By solidifying expectations for higher Fed interest rates to fight inflation, the surge in oil
prices has contributed to the spike in long-term Treasury yields this year. According to an
October 13, 2026, Empirical Research Partners report, the Iran-war oil crisis is estimated
to have contributed about 0.50 pp to the roughly 2.40 pp increase in the real U.S. long-
term Treasury rate from 2020 to early October (Exhibit 1A). According to the report, this

oil-price effect accounts for “much of the rise in real rates since February 27, 2026...”
On the positive side, while most other underpinnings for higher rates compared to the
pre-pandemic decade are structural (Exhibit 1A), the oil-price-related effect on interest
rates is reversible, implying that a resolution to the current oil crisis has the potential to
help lower both inflation and interest rates, relieving some of the current headwinds to
growth and asset prices.

Indeed, while much of the increase in interest rates since the pandemic reflects stronger,
more normal economic growth, and has thus been accompanied by surging corporate
1 Summary of Commentary on Current Economic Conditions by Federal Reserve District, Federal Reserve, October 2026.
Investment Implications
Oil market conditions remain a swing
factor for the outlook. A shift lower in
oil risk premia and improvement on
the refining front would go a long way

toward easing inflation and interest
rates while bolstering risk-asset
appetite. A gradual increase in Fixed
Income portfolio duration continues to
make sense, in our view.

3 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
earnings and risk-asset prices, worsening oil-market conditions since mid-summer and
associated spike in yields have been accompanied by a loss of momentum in U.S. equity
prices (Exhibit 1B). Given their higher sensitivity to energy price and interest rates shocks,
Small-caps lost relatively more momentum than Large-caps during this period.
The Large-cap rally also faded, though, as oil and interest rate headwinds widened stock-

level return dispersion, with roughly 40% of constituent stocks in bear market territory
(down 20% or more). Still, buoyed by the Technology sector as well as by expectations
of conflict resolution, the market-capitalization weighted S&P 500 Index has mainly
consolidated just below its peak. While the equal-weighted S&P 500 Index stumbled more,
it was just about 3% off its peak as of October 20.
While the economy remains resilient and profit expectations remain strong, persistent

oil market turmoil makes the macro backdrop more fragile. For example, the Beige Book
not only highlights concern about spreading fuel-related inflation, as noted above, but its
language also suggests that economic activity lost some momentum since its mid-July
to late-August report. Overall, it describes growth as slight to moderate, with generally
moderate manufacturing growth except for robust activity in the energy-heavy Dallas
District. Little growth is reported in other sectors overall, with most Districts seeing little

to no change in consumer spending, for example.
Employment was described as stable to little changed, with generally adequate labor supply
except for certain specialized trade workers. Five of twelve Districts note some reports of
businesses cutting staff through attrition, delaying hiring or anticipating less job creation
as a result of AI-driven productivity gains. Most Districts reported that business maintained
a positive outlook for coming months, though “expectations tend to be for slight growth,

weighted toward manufacturing and burdened by persistent uncertainty.”
The September employment and retail sales reports seem to validate the anecdotal Beige
Book evidence. Also, the 0.4% annualized manufacturing production growth over the July to
September period (and 1.1% YoY, according to the Federal Reserve Broad), while welcome,
is hardly an indication of booming conditions. Against a backdrop of rising electricity prices,
wage growth lagging inflation, and higher gasoline prices broadly offsetting this year’s tax

cut benefits, real consumer spending is unlikely to accelerate much absent stronger labor
demand and real wage growth. A resolution to the oil crisis that brings economic relief and
tempers interest rates is thus critical for continued expansion in 2027, in our view.

Investment Implications
Portfolio maintenance is a key
component of successful investing.
It’s time for investors to assess their
portfolios—or look under the hood.
MARKET VIEW
Six Questions Investors Should Be Asking Themselves Right Now
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
After four years of rising equity prices, investors have plenty to celebrate. Despite

inflation scares, geopolitical tensions, trade wars, rising interest rates and breathless
predictions around AI, the S&P 500 has returned some 131% (total returns) since the
bull market in equities commenced in October 2022. Corporate earnings hav e generally
exceeded expectations; AI has ignited a historic investment boom, and America’s
technology giants have powered the market to new heights.
We believe the bull will rumble on into 2027. We also believe that now is the time for

investors to take stock of their portfolios, since bull markets have a way of changing the
composition of portfolios. In addition, investors should not assume that the investment
strategies that worked well in the past four years will necessarily deliver comparable
results during the next four. Below are six questions investors should be asking
themselves right now:
Number One: How concentrated is my portfolio? Many investors believe they are
invested in the stock market when, in fact, they are invested in a handful of companies—

specifically AI-related names that have soared and dominated the index over the past
few years. Information technology now accounts for roughly 40% of the market cap of
the S&P 500, while the top 10 companies represent a share of 38.4%.
As the Financial Times recently noted, U.S. equity and credit markets have become so
increasingly concentrated in AI-related groups that “more than tw o-thirds of companies
in the Russell 1000 index of large U.S. groups are linked to AI, either directly or

indirectly or because the technology is becoming embedded in their business models.” 2
Meanwhile, Big Technology hyperscalers and companies that are beneficiaries of the AI
spending boom make up about 16% of the U.S. high-grade bond market after a huge
borrowing binge, making the grouping the largest in the investment-grade bond market. 2
The upshot: Investors that have been rewarded by concentration need to ask themselves
what percentage of their wealth is tied to the all-encompassing AI trade and consider

what would happen if mega-cap tech leadership falters.
Number Two: How diversified is my portfolio? While concentration asks whether
investors own too much of a few assets, diversification asks whether investors own enough
of different assets that behave differently when economic cycles or financial conditions
change. A portfolio containing hundreds of U.S. Equities via exchange-traded funds, mutual
funds and other assets may appear diversified. But if those companies are overwhelmingly

exposed to the same economic cycle, interest rate environment, or technology theme,
the diversification benefits are diluted. Case in point: While we remain constructive on
Emerging Market (EM) Equities, investors need to understand that a significant driver of
EM returns has come from AI-driven markets like Taiwan and South Korea.
Today, true diversification means considering exposure to hard assets (commodities, real
estate), EM mining and agricultural leaders, EM bonds and currencies, China bio-technology,

Small-cap international, and Private Equity and Hedge Funds for qualified investors.
Number Three: How tax-efficient is my portfolio? Bull markets create wealth—in
addition to tax liabilities. The good news is that after four years of rising equity returns,
investors are sitting on sizable, unrealized capital gains. The bad news: Market gains
can feel like a financial straitjacket, making it difficult to rebalance portfolios without
triggering significant tax liabilities.

Against this backdrop, now is the time for investors to review where the assets are
held—distinguishing between taxable and tax-advantaged accounts, and the attendant tax
ramifications. The appropriate mix depends on individual circumstances, including future
2 “Investors seek shelter from heightened exposure to AI”, The Financial Times, October 6, 2026.

### Page 5

5 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
withdrawal taxes. It is also time to consider tax-loss strategies, charitable donations of
securities and other tactics and strategies since in the end, the measure of inv estment
success isn’t simply how much money investors make. It’s how much they keep.
Number Four: Is it time to take profits?* There is a peculiarity to bull markets: The
more money investors make, the more the attendant tax liabilities. Tax liabilities, in turn,

can discourage selling, and the reluctance to sell can leave investors dangerously exposed
to yesterday’s leaders and compound concentration risks. That brings up an emotionally
difficult decision: When should investors sell their leaders or take some chips off the table?
The question is notably relevant today, given four years of substantial Equity gains.
Beyond tax considerations, investors need to understand that profit-taking can create
financial flexibility. Realized after-tax gains can replenish cash reserves, fund near-

term spending needs, reduce leverage or most importantly, provide capital to rebalance
portfolios in other areas of the market. In general, the goal of taking profits isn’t to get
out of the market—it is to ensure that after years of accumulated gains, these proceeds
are redeployed and redistributed across various asset classes that continue to support
and underpin long-term wealth accumulation.
Number Five: How well is my portfolio positioned for a new interest rate

regime? For much of the past quarter century, investors operated in a world of copious
liquidity, declining interest rates and relatively cheap capital. The era of capital abundance
favored stocks over bonds, but that era is over—we are shifting to a world of capital
scarcity, or intense competition for capital and the attendant hike in the cost of capital.
In this world, higher rates weigh the most on long-duration assets. Positioning for this
regime favors companies with strong balance sheets and cash flow over firms with heavy

debt or refinancing needs. High-quality bonds are more attractiv e in this environment;
sovereign debt risks are concentrated more overseas (i.e., France) than the U.S.
Number Six: How well is my portfolio positioned for the next shock? Shocks
happen. If the past four years have taught investors anything, it is that the unexpected
should be the expected. This decade alone has produced gut-wrenching market mo ves
related to a global pandemic, a ground war in Europe, the reconfiguration of global

supply chains, epic technological change and a war in the Middle East. Through it all, U.S.
Equities have trended higher but hardly in a linear fashion.
The next shocks could emerge in the global bond market, where mounting go vernment
debt and higher borrowing costs have triggered mounting volatility. It could emerge from
disappointing returns on massive AI investments. Or it could come from a geopolitical
event, another energy shock or any other unforeseen event. Investors can’t predict the

next shock, but they can prepare for it. So now is the time for investors to stress test
their portfolios against a potential shock, examining how a steep downdraft in Equities
or spike in bond yields would affect their retirement plans, liquidity needs and other
financial goals and objectives.
Exhibit 2: Six Questions to Stress Test Your Portfolio.
The six questions What investors should examine Portfolio diagnostic
1. How concentrated am I? Mega-cap technology exposure and overlapping holdings % of portfolio in top 10 stocks

2. Am I truly diversified? Asset allocation, geographic mix and correlated exposures Current equity/bond mix versus target
3. How tax-efficient am I? Unrealized gains, asset location and tax-loss harvesting Embedded capital gains and estimated tax cost of rebalancing
4. Is it time to take profits? Portfolio drift, oversized winners and valuations Positions exceeding target allocation
5. Am I ready for higher interest rates? Duration, leverage, refinancing risks and cash flows Bond duration and exposure to highly leveraged companies

6. Am I prepared for the next shock? Liquidity, downside exposure and portfolio resilience Estimated loss in a 20% equity selloff
Source: Chief Investment Office. Data as of October 22, 2026.
* “Pop the Champagne,” Portfolio Insights, Chief Investment Office, August 2026.

6 of 8 October 26, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THE TICKER TAPE
What are rising credit spreads signaling about the broader market environment?
Christopher Hyzy, Chief Investment Officer
Rising French bond yields with a sharp rise in spreads above German bunds, mixed
with AI buildout concerns, are creating nervousness across the capital markets. This
nervousness centers around the ability to fund deficits, the potential for slower growth

and the future return on the announced capital investment. We expect these concerns
to continue to linger well into next year as growth worries remain in France, bond
yields stay sticky around current levels, and, in the case of AI, investors demand more
compensation for financing.
At this stage, we do not expect the rising spreads in targeted areas to be a precursor
for a material slowdown in economic growth overall. Rather, we believe this is a natural

signal that investors are moving up the quality stack as we move into the middle of
the AI buildout. The bottom line is that rising credit spreads in lower quality corners of
the market are natural along the lifecycle of financing. When free cash flow is called
into question, or when debt financing costs begin to approach the expected return on
investment, investors begin to reprice risk.
We continue to emphasize higher quality balance sheets in corporate and sovereign

credit as well as higher quality leaders in the AI buildout overall. The closer we get to
2027, investors will likely begin to transition from too much exposure to the “buildout”
and not enough exposure to “AI adopters.”

Index Definitions
Securities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
SS&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

Consumer Price Index is a statistical measure that tracks the average change over time in the prices paid by urban consumers for a representative "market basket" of everyday goods and
services.
S&P 500 Market-cap Weighted Index is a float-adjusted market-capitalization-weighted index, meaning a company's influence and percentage weight in the index are determined by its
total market value (share price multiplied by the number of outstanding shares).

S&P 500 Equal-Weighted Index is a variation of the traditional S&P 500 where every single company in the index is given the exact same weight—0.2%—regardless of its size or market
capitalization.
S&P 500 Equal-Weighted Industrials Index imposes equal weights on the index constituents included in the S&P 500 that are classified in the GICS® industrials sector.
Russell 1000 Index is a stock market index that tracks the performance of the 1,000 largest publicly traded companies in the United States.

This material does not take into account a client’s particular investment objectives, financial situations, or needs and is not intended as a recommendation, offer, or solicitation for the purchase
or sale of any security or investment strategy. Merrill offers a broad range of brokerage, investment advisory and other services. There are important differences between brokerage and

of America, Merrill or any affiliate to sell or provide, or a specific invitation for a consumer to apply for, any particular retail financial product or service that may be available.
The Chief Investment Office (“CIO”) provides thought leadership on wealth management, investment strategy and global markets; portfolio management solutions; due diligence; and solutions
oversight and data analytics. CIO viewpoints are developed for Bank of America Private Bank, a division of Bank of America, N.A., (“Bank of America”) and Merrill Lynch, Pierce, Fenner & Smith

The Global Wealth & Investment Management Investment Strategy Committee (“GWIM ISC”) is responsible for developing and coordinating recommendations for short-term and long-term
investment strategy and market views encompassing markets, economic indicators, asset classes and other market-related projections affecting GWIM.
BofA Global Research is research produced by BofA Securities, Inc. (“BofAS”) and/or one or more of its affiliates. BofAS is a registered broker-dealer, Member SIPC and wholly owned subsidiary

of Bank of America Corporation.
All recommendations must be considered in the context of an individual investor’s goals, time horizon, liquidity needs and risk tolerance. Not all recommendations will be in the best interest
of all investors.
Asset allocation, diversification and rebalancing do not ensure a profit or protect against loss in declining markets.
Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

動態更新  科技‧美國

Vertiv 
(VRT.N/ VRT US)

Onecore、CDU與 800VDC為三大推升營運加速成長之動能 
重要訊息 
Vertiv  FY3Q26 獲利優於市場共識 21%，公司上修 FY2026 EPS 財測約 7%
至年增 70%，來自資料中心散熱與電源需求升溫， 收盤股價上漲約 16%。 
評論及分析  
FY3Q26 EPS 年增 78%至 2.21 美元、優於市場共識 22%。Vertiv  FY3Q26 
(2026 年 7-9 月)營收年增 61%、季增 32%至 43.1 億美元，主要受惠資料
中心需求升溫，以及 FY2Q26 因供應端問題遞延之營收約 1-1.5 億美元已於
本季認列。FY3Q26 營利率年增 2.9ppts、季增 2.6ppts 至 25.2%，主要受
惠營利率較佳的美洲地區成長較強勁 ；EPS 年增 78%、季增 45%至 2.21 美
元，優於凱基預估 15%、市場共識預估 22%。 
Vertiv 上修 FY2026 EPS 財測約 7%至 7.15 美元。Vertiv  展望 FY4Q26 營
收年增 53%、季增 2%至 44.1 億美元，若還原 FY3Q26 遞延營收之貢獻，
則兩個季度的營收年增率相當； EPS 財測為年增 65%、季增 2%至 2.25 美
元，大致符合市場共識預估。基於資料中心需求升溫， 公司上調 FY2026 營
收約 5%至 146.5 億美元，隱含年增 43%；EPS 財測上修 7%至 7.15 美
元，隱含年增 70%，優於市場共識預估 7%。 
凱基預估 Vertiv  FY2026/2027 EPS 為 7.32/11.89 美元，年增 62%。
Vertiv 在 2026/10 宣布其 2.3MW CDU 取得 Nvidia 認證，供應鏈評估單台售
價將較 1.3MW 提升 50%，每建置 1GW 資料中心的 CDU 產值約達 2 億美
元，而目前僅三家供應商通過認證 ，凱基預期此將有助於公司提升 CDU 供
貨份額，並據此上修 FY2026/FY2027 EPS 自原預估之 6.91/10.94 美元至
7.32/11.89 美元，年增 62%。 
投資建議  
評等維持「增加持股」，目標價 416 美元 (35x FY2027F EPS) 。 
投資風險 
AI 晶片訂單遞延； CSP 廠放緩資本支出增幅；關稅政策變化影響生產成本。

### Page 2

美國 Vertiv

十月 29, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  2

Vertiv 營收主要來自資料中心基礎建設解決方案 。Vertiv 業務為提供資料中
心基礎建設解決方案， 營收約 75%來自資料中心客戶。Vertiv 產品組合可分
為硬體 (Products ； FY2Q26 營收占比 80%) 及服務與備品 (Services & 
Spares；20%) 兩大項目。其中  (1) 硬體：提供電源管理、熱管理及整合性
機櫃解決方案，包括交流不斷電供應系統 (UPS)、冷卻系統等；(2) 服務與備
品包含預防性維護、工程和諮詢服務、遠程監控、備件等。FY 2Q26 各地區
營收比重為美洲  68%、亞太地區  19%、歐非中東地區 13%。獲利方面，
美洲基於 AI 資料中心規格要求較高 ，營利率 達 25%-30%、優於公司平
均；歐洲近幾季因關稅成本上揚而使營利率降至 15%-20%；亞太地區因價
格競爭激烈，營利率僅約 10%-15%、低於平均。  
Vertiv 提供完整電源與散熱系統 解決方案，協助客戶建置資料中心 。相較於
散熱或電源廠同業大多僅提供零組件， Vertiv 擁有完整產品線與提供系統建
置的能力；基於下一代 AI 語言模型的運算量與傳輸需求皆顯著提升，使散
熱整合與解決方案的設計難度也隨之向上，讓 Vertiv 相較於僅能提供單一零
組件的廠商更有競爭力。 另外，因應資料中心停機造成損失的問題，Vertiv
也推出 Next Predict ，透過即時遙測與 AI 演算法持續監控 BESS 電池儲能系
統、UPS 與液冷 CDU/泵浦的運作狀態，在異常影響 AI 算力前先行預警，從
而預防損失。Vertiv 資料中心客戶包含 AWS、Meta、Microsoft 、AT&T、
Verizon 、Coreweave 、Oklo，供應模式大多是將電源、散熱與系統等產品
一併提供給客戶，協助其建置資料中心。  
在產品接單上，凱基預期 Vertiv 推出之 OneCore 資料中心建置解決方案將
對整體訂單具有高度貢獻。此解決方案主要是因應伺服器液冷散熱與電源需
求所提供的產品，其採用直接接觸晶片散熱 (Direct -to-Chip Cooling) 技術，
避免晶片在電力故障的幾十秒內因失去冷卻而受損 ，提供建置容量從 10MW
到 1,000MW 的選擇。OneCore 資料中心建置解決方案 之中的 MegaMod ™ 
HDX 主要是針對需要建造一座小型資料中心的客戶所設計，因應數十櫃至數
百櫃的 AI 資料中心建置需求。基於 AI 伺服器機房的液冷散熱管路與電力分
配較為複雜，若由不同供應商安裝可 能會出現漏水或電力供應不足的狀況；
Vertiv 可解決此整合問題，並將安裝時間壓縮在 9-12 個月，相較於大多數
供應商須 18-24 個月而言更為迅速。 目前市場上已有超過 500 家公司有自
建小型資料中心的需求，若以 10MW 方案價格約 8,000 萬美元評估，潛在
市場產值達 400 億美元以上，而觀察目前有提供同等級產品者僅
Schneider( 法)與 Eaton  (美)等同業，使 Vertiv 可望在此領域成為主要供應商
之一， 凱基預期公司 將取得超過 130 億美元訂單，有利於公司
FY2026/FY20 27 營收維持高成長性。  
Vertiv 在 2026/10 宣布其 2.3MW CDU 取得 Nvidia 認證，供應鏈評估單台售
價將較 1.3MW 提升 50%，每建置 1GW 資料中心的 CDU 產值約達 2 億美
元，目前具相同提供能力的供應商僅包含韓國 LG 電子與美國 LiquidStack ，
Vertiv 可望在 CDU 供應鏈提升份額，預期公司可在此市場取得約 20%供貨
份額，並可望貢獻 2027 年營收達 15 億美元。

### Page 3

美國 Vertiv

十月 29, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  3

圖 1: Vertiv推出 2.3MW CDU，預估貢獻 2027年營收達 15億美元

資料來源：公司資料；凱基

Nvidia 與 ASIC AI 伺服器陸續導入電源櫃，預計 FY2027 顯著貢獻營收。
基於 Nvidia 與 CSP 廠下世代 AI 伺服器功耗皆顯著提升，客戶對於能處理達
200kW 熱能的液冷散熱需求增加，推升散熱產值提升。另外，因各地政府
對 PUE 規範逐步往 1.5 以下的水準靠攏，而現行氣冷散熱之 PUE 普遍落在
1.7 上下的水準，此限制亦使客戶對散熱方案產生升級需求。凱基 預期在 AI
散熱解決方案升級、MegaMod ™ HDX 營收貢獻逐步增加、以及公司可望在
2.3MW CDU 市場提升份額的帶動下，Vertiv FY2026/FY2027 散熱產品營收
可望達 60.9/92.0 億美元，年增 51%。 
電源產品部分，Vertiv  800VDC 產品目前處於驗證階段 ，預計 2027 年開始
貢獻營收，合作對象包含 hyperscaler 及晶片端夥伴。基於其單價約達 50 萬
美元、預估 2027 年出貨量約落在 1,000- 2,000 櫃上下的水準；公司目前亦
有推出 400VDC 電源櫃，初期以 Meta 和 Microsoft 導入較為積極，預期電
源櫃產品可望貢獻 Vertiv  FY2027 營收達 10 億美元，為其 2027 年電源產品
線主要成長動能，預估 Vertiv FY2026/FY2027 電源產品營收為 36.5/60.7 億
美元，年增 66%。

*財年止於十二月

Vertiv 評等維持「增加持股」，目標價為 416 美元。Vertiv 在資料中心散熱
和電源產品提供的 solution 比大多數同業更完整，營收成長性可望優於產業
平均；凱基預期公司推出的 OneCore 資料中心建置解決方案將拿下超過
130 億美元訂單， 此為支撐公司營運成長之主要動能。另外，公司 在
2026/10 宣布其 2.3MW CDU 取得 Nvidia 認證，供應鏈評估單台售價將較
1.3MW 提升 50%，每建置 1GW 資料中心的 CDU 產值約達 2 億美元，而目
前僅三家供應商通過認證，凱基預期此將有助於公司提升 CDU 供貨份額，
並據此上修 FY2026/FY2027 EPS 自原預估之 6.91/10.94 美元至 7.32/11.89
美元，年增 62%；相較市場共識 EPS 分別為 6.66/9.23 美元，目前股價交易
於約 22/26 倍之 FY2027 凱基與市場共識 EPS 預估。 
Vertiv 過往五年本益比多落於 12-36 倍之間，考量 (1) Vertiv 在電源與散熱
產品之整合能力優於同業表現，並能提供系統級產品，有助公司接單能 力提
升；(2) ASIC 晶片陸續推出且對電源與散熱需求皆提升，可望推升公司未來
兩年獲利成長性優於產業平均；(3) 公司在 2.3MW CDU 與 800VDC 領域皆
為少數供應商之一， FY2027 電源與散熱產品成長性可望進一步提升。綜合
上述原因，預期 Vertiv 本益比將回升至區間上緣之 35 倍上下浮動；基於當
前評價偏低，故維持「增加持股」之 評等，目標價則基於預估 EPS 上修而調
升至 416 美元 (35x FY2027F EPS )。

圖 5: 公司概況  圖 6: 公司 2025年美洲營收占比達 62% 
Vertiv 前身為 Emerson Network Power ，並於 2016 年開始獨
立運營。公司專注於為資料中心、通訊網路 以及商業與工業環
境提供關鍵基礎設施與服務。產品與服務涵蓋交流與直流電源
管理、不間斷電源系統（ UPS）、電源分配單元（PDU ）、熱
管理解決方案、資料中心基礎設施管理（ DCIM）以及服務
等。2025 年各地區營收比重為美洲 62%、亞太 20%、歐非
中東 18%。

Document 358: Eurozone_ Amid rising inflation, the business climate remains resilient, but consumer confidence falls back (2026-10-02)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Eurozone_ Amid rising inflation, the business climate remains resilient, but consumer confidence falls back (2026-10-02).pdf

### Page 1

September 2026: Marked improvement in
services business confidence
September 2026: Business climate in the
manufacturing sector comfortably in the
expansion zone for the 8th month in a row

pressures
September 2026: Slight further increase in

to a 3-year high
Eurozone: Amid rising inflation, the business climate remains resilient, but consumer

confidence falls back
02/10/2026 3
We have selected two inflation measures (with and without energy) and six survey indicators: 1/ & 2/ business confidence, as
measured by the PMIs in the manufacturing and services sectors; 3/ & 4/ input and output prices components of the
composite PMI (in order to identify direct inflationary pressures); 5/ the suppliers’ delivery times component of the
manufacturing PMI (a direct indicator of possible supply difficulties and supply-demand imbalance, and therefore, indirectly,

of inflationary pressures in the making); and 6/ consumer confidence (measured by the “assessment of financial situation in
the next 12 months” in order to capture the impact of inflation on purchasing power). The trends in each of these indicators
are observed relative to month m=0, corresponding to the start of the conflict. Each line does not represent the level of the
indicator, but its cumulative variation compared to month m=0.
The overall picture from the confidence surveys available for September is globally positive. According to the PMIs, the

business climate in services has indeed seen a clear improvement, and that in the manufacturing sector remains comfortably
in expansion territory for the 8 th month in a row. However, consumer confidence has recorded its first decline in 5 months,
likely driven by rising inflation and concerns over purchasing power. News on the inflation front is negative. PMI surveys
report a strengthening of inflationary pressures: this trend is not a surprise given the tensions in energy prices, but it is

nonetheless significant. As for inflation itself, it continues to rise, more markedly in September: +0.6 percentage points, to
3.8% year-on-year (Eurostat flash estimate), of which 0.4 pp du to the higher energy contribution. Inflation excluding energy
increased as well, by 0.2 pp to 2.3% (of which 0.1 pp due to the rise in food inflation).
Source: S&P Global, Eurostat, European Commission, Macrobond, BNP Paribas
ECONOMIC RESEARCH
Change since m = 0 = February 2026 / February 2022.
